### 2026-11-19 — `session-turn-and-composer` (backlog item 1u): the session's turn and the composer, with the plain states

**Why.** Measured 2026-11-19 at the frozen revision `b375fac9`: the old session screen's motion was invented (a sent message showed the pending line for 7 ticks, then a canned `reply()`, `main.html:3439`; the play's lines were fixed markup), no protocol event name appeared in the file, and its data had no CLI-shaped key. The Mac app's `session` module is 15 files and 6,164 lines, and the Mac app holds 44 recorded `.jsonl` streams. 1f, the whole session screen, was split before the run into this part and 1v; 1u lays down the session module every chat preset draws on: one turn grown only from the CLI's recorded machine-mode streams, replayed through 1d's stand-in, the composer, the header, the answer's markdown and code, the capture script, and the four presets that need no scenario.

- [x] **The session module** (`lib/design/prototype/source/presentation/session/`), the Mac app's files and names:
  - `ViewModels/Transcript.ts`: the pure reducer, `apply(SessionEvent)`, into `TranscriptItem`s -- `user`, the turn's `activity` (reasoning and tool lines), its `answer` (exactly its deltas joined, finished by its result, with the model the result names), `notice`, `stopped`, `ended` and the quiet `unknown` line; `tool_call` a named skip; the person's acts `userSent` and `cancelling`; `userWroteAhead`, a still preset's send drawn where the child reads it;
  - `ViewModels/SessionViewModel.ts`: connection, transcript, draft, send, Stop, the `/` and `@` menus, attach and detach, the model and thinking menus (a `set` line, held while a turn runs), the context meter, private mode, the suite chooser and Switch, `inspectorOpen`, the `leave` intent with the `endPrivate` confirm, and `face()`, everything its views draw;
  - `ViewModels/SessionActions.ts` (`SlashCommand`, `ContextMeter`, `SuiteChoice`, `PrivateMode`, `HeldLine`, `AttachmentKind`) and `ViewModels/Play.ts` (`PlayNarration`, `PlayChoice`);
  - `Views/`: `SessionWindow`, `TranscriptRow` (with `ActivityView` and `QuietLine`), `InputBar`, `SuiteSheet` and `SessionExtras` (`EmptySessionView`, `MeterView`, `AttachmentChips`, `SuggestionList`, `ModelGlyph`, `ModelTag`, and `InspectorPlaceholder`, the inspector's 304 px column until 1g);
  - `Previews/`: `SessionWindow.preview.tsx` (23 states), `TranscriptRow.preview.tsx` (13), `InputBar.preview.tsx` (12), `SuiteSheet.preview.tsx` (5), `SessionExtras.preview.tsx` (15), over `SessionPreview.ts` (`useSessionPreview`); `SessionPresets.ts`, the four presets, and `sessionSamples`.
- [x] **The markdown and code component** (`source/presentation/styles/Components/`, the Mac's names): `MarkdownDocument.ts` (blocks and spans, an open fence read as code mid-stream), `MarkdownText.tsx` (the old answer's look: a fence as its captioned card with copy), `CodeLexer.ts` (the Mac's 19 languages and their aliases in the five `code.*` colors, a diff by line, an unknown language plain), with `styles/Previews/MarkdownText.preview.tsx` (5 states) and `Styles.ts` exporting them.
- [x] **Business reads** (`source/business/reads/`), where the Mac app keeps them: `Models.ts` (`Field`, `ModelKind`, `ModelRow`, `ModelInfo.window`), `SuiteFootprint.ts` (`SuiteFootprint` from the footprint's text, `Suites.names`) and `Symphonies.ts` (`Symphony.list`).
- [x] **The stand-in** (`source/business/session/MachineSession.ts`): a send to a child that refused its launch is no longer logged as a turn past its recording (decision below).
- [x] **The composition root** (`source/presentation/app/`): `AppViewModel` builds `ApogeeClient` and the session; reads the model facts, a new or private chat's default suite and the suites, and hands them in; carries a leave out (a new or a private session, opened on Sessions with any overlay closed, as the old `sessionGo`); and `start()` awaits a chat preset's setup before the clock starts. `Presets.ts` gains `chat:new`, `chat:private`, `chat:play` and `chat:suite`; `ApogeeApp.tsx` hands Sessions `SessionWindow`, the sheet host the suite chooser and the confirm host the `endPrivate` words; its previews gain the four presets and the confirm (5 more, 8 in all).
- [x] **The sweep of 1f:** `shell/ViewModels/Destination.ts` loses `sessions: '1f'` (Sessions is ported); `AppViewModel.ts`'s and `MonitorViewModel.ts`'s Unload comments name 1u and 1v; the Presentation card's `session` entry and Position line; `KeyMap.test.ts`'s placeholder list; `e2e/opens-from-disk.spec.ts`'s double-click and places tests; the default start's comment and test title now say 1v. `MILESTONES.md`'s mention of `session-screen.md` (1e's record) is left as shipped.
- [x] **The capture script** (`scripts/capture.ts`, run as `npm run capture -- <recipe>`, not part of `check`) with its tests (`scripts/capture.test.ts`, a fake binary) and its actor files (`scripts/actors/play-root.json`, `play-helper.json`); `package.json` gains the `capture` script.
- [x] **The capture:** `fixtures/data/protocol/execute-play-summarize-verify.jsonl` (13 lines), `chat:play`'s stream.
- [x] **The module map:** `presentation/session` gains `infrastructure/clock`, `infrastructure/process`, `data/protocol`, `business/reads` and `business/session`; `presentation/app` gains `business/session`.
- [x] **Tests:** 12 new unit files (the session's `Play`, `Transcript`, `SessionActions`, `Replays` and `SessionExtras` (the views' drawing: the chips' glyphs and the unlaunched empty state's acts), the styles' `CodeLexer`, `MarkdownDocument` and `MarkdownText`, the reads' `Models`, `SuiteFootprint` and `Symphonies`, and `scripts/capture.test.ts`) and 6 changed (`SessionViewModel`, from the stub's one test to 29; `AppViewModel`, 11 to 29; `Presets`, its walk pressing the suite control wherever the face offers it; `MachineSession`; `KeyMap`; `Styles`); a layering test planting React in the session's view-model and `sessions` in `session`; a token-scan test holding the lexer's colors to `var(--code-…)`; 8 new Playwright tests (the eighth, Choose a suite on `chat:new`, from the review's fixes) and 3 changed (the double-click, places and theme tests).
- [x] **`lib/design/main.html`**, rebuilt: 672,115 bytes (`dist/main.html 672.11 kB │ gzip: 319.25 kB`; was 544,677).
- [x] **Docs:** `DEVELOPER.md` (a new section, "The session", with "The capture script"; the layout, commands, module map, "When the clock starts", the composition root and "Opening a preset"); `assistant/design/CLAUDE.md` (where the design stands, the codebase map); the Presentation and Business layer cards; the queue (this document and its row deleted; 1v opened, 🟢, in the index, the overview and the ROADMAP; the overview's counts, 19 to build and 15 of them the design's, and "can start today", design 1v in 1u's place; 1s's "Waits on"); 1v's, 1g's, 1p's and 1o's documents re-pointed at `DEVELOPER.md`'s section and Milestone A, with a dated hand-off note in 1v's and 1g's (both brought up to the review's fixes: the default suite's read at open, a leave going to Sessions, the unlaunched session's acts off, and the Switch relaunch's recording).

**The streams, each recorded or captured; none hand-made.**
- Read in place from the Mac app's recordings (`lib/src/darwin/tests/…/Fixtures/`): `new-unsent.jsonl` (`chat:new`, `chat:suite`), `private-unsent.jsonl` (`chat:private`, and "Private session instead"), and, in the previews and tests, `markdown-answer.jsonl`, `code-answer.jsonl`, `stopped-turn.jsonl`, `execute-play.jsonl`, `execute-project.jsonl`, `chat-under-suite.jsonl`, `chat-under-suite-m29.jsonl`, `set-suite-refused.jsonl` and `mock-actor.jsonl`; the reads `suites-models-list.json`, `models-info-switched-claude.json`, `suites-models-info-root.json`, `symphonies-list.json`, `suites-config-get.txt`, `suites-default-suite.txt` and `suites-models-status-{cloud,fast,pair,shared,vast}.json`.
- Captured from the branch build for `chat:play`: `TMPDIR=<session scratchpad> npm run capture -- execute-play-summarize-verify`, which printed `capture: fixtures/data/protocol/execute-play-summarize-verify.jsonl from apogee 0.2.0 (5bc19144, 2026-11-19, macos-arm64)`. The binary was `lib/src/cli/build/macos-arm64/source/apogee`, in a throwaway home and working folder (`ingest`, seeded with an `AGENTS.md` of 12 words and `minutes.py`, 95 bytes in all), on two mock members, `root` (chat) and `helper` (utility), under the suite `coding` with `--orchestrate on`, playing `summarize-verify` on the old frame's line. The capture's temp folder was removed after it.
- Planted lines exist only in tests: an unknown type and a line that is not JSON (the quiet line), a `tool_call` (the named skip), and a result carrying `usage` (no recorded stream reports one).

Decisions the item recorded before the build:
- 2026-11-14: **The user's call:** the prototype is rebuilt in React, TypeScript and Vite with the production GUIs' four layers and MVVM modules, built into one self-contained `lib/design/main.html`, in v0.2.1.
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** data is the CLI's recorded files read in place; a state nobody recorded gets a hand-made file in the CLI's exact shape, named `*-handmade*`.
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** parity with the frozen old prototype is judged on layout, states, controls and wording, not pixels or data.
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** the old prototype is frozen (fixes only) from 1c's baseline; new design is drawn in the React app now that 1e has shipped.
- 2026-11-14: 1f was marked **split first** into this part and 1v.
- 2026-11-19: **The split** (the user's answer before the group run, 2026-11-18): this part is 1f's first, gated on 1e, which had shipped.
- 2026-11-19: **The ids** (the run owner's ruling, for veto): 1f stays spent; this part is 1u, 1v follows it, both ahead of 1g.
- 2026-11-19: **The comparison is scoped to the main pane** (the run owner's ruling, for veto): the header, the transcript and the composer, and the suite sheet over `chat:suite`; the session list and the inspector at 1g; the whole window at 1s.
- 2026-11-19: **The header's inspector button and the `endPrivate` confirm are this part's** (the run owner's ruling, for veto).
- 2026-11-19: **The freeze at a named event is 1v's** (the run owner's ruling): none of this part's presets stands mid-turn.
- 2026-11-19: **The markdown default's list grows; its choice does not** (flagged for veto): the component draws every block `markdown-answer.jsonl` carries, with no new package; an image is its alt text and its address, never fetched; a link is followed only for http, https and mailto.
- 2026-11-19: **Captures use the branch build** in a throwaway `APOGEE_HOME`, never an installed binary or a real home.

The open calls' defaults, taken as stated (2026-11-18):
- **Each missing stream recorded with `capture-fixture.sh`'s recipe** against the mock backend, from the branch build. `chat:play`'s capture ran (above), so no hand-made stream was needed. Its counts are the old frame's own -- "AGENTS.md, 12 words", "attaching . (2 files, 95 bytes)", "graph: 2 nodes, 1 edge (supported: python 1; skipped: .md 1)" -- since the seeded folder was sized to them; the differences that remain are listed below.
- **The capture script lives beside the prototype;** the Mac app's `capture-fixture.sh` and `mock-actor.jsonl` are unchanged.
- **The markdown and code view is the prototype's own `styles` component,** with no new package; the unnamed and elixir fences draw uncolored.
- **A still chat preset's send plays:** `AppViewModel.start()` awaits the session preset's setup (locate, open, and the preset's sends) before it starts the clock. **This branch was taken;** the alternative (extending 1d's still-frame catch-up to a send written after `start()`) was not needed.
- **The inspector button sets `inspectorOpen`** (open by default, as the old `inspector: true`); the context meter's click opens it; `SessionWindow` reserves an open inspector's column with `InspectorPlaceholder`, an `aside` at `flex: 0 0 304px` on `--surface-inspector` with the left border, saying "The inspector isn't drawn here yet. Design item 1g ports it."
- **The `endPrivate` confirm is built in this module** (named, not followed: the Mac raises it from `sessions`). `SessionViewModel.leave(then)` asks only when a private session has a turn sent, in the old words and both bodies, through 1e's `ConfirmHost`; it is reached from its tests and the app's `EndPrivate` preview. "Private session instead" calls the intent. The app root's door for 1p is `AppViewModel.leave`.
- **`SessionViewModel` takes the model facts by injection;** `AppViewModel` reads `models list` once and `models info <model>` for the `session` event's model (and again for an answer by another model), and each preset carries those two read rules. A sub line or window the CLI does not report is drawn as the CLI says it.
- **While `monitor` is the default start (until 1v), Sessions shows the session window's empty state and launches nothing;** `monitor`'s per-preset test answers every call with no chat rule.
- **The `@` menu lists the old prototype's `~/code/ingest` listing,** injected where the Mac's `Mentions.candidates` reads a folder. The document said eight files; the old `repoFiles()` (`main.html:3846`) lists ten, and all ten are used (the menu shows at most seven, as the old one does).

Decisions taken while building (2026-11-19, an unattended group run), each for veto:
- **The user's answers before the run:** the npm registry and Playwright's Chromium were allowed; neither was needed (`package.json`'s dependencies and the lock file are unchanged; `~/Library/Caches/ms-playwright` still holds `chromium-1248`, `chromium_headless_shell-1248` and `ffmpeg-1013`). A Playwright test opening the built file from `file://` stood in for your look. **You try the file afterwards** (Not done).
- **The capture's members are named `root` and `helper`,** the names the recorded `suites-models-list.json` and `suites-models-info-root.json` carry, so `chat:play`'s header reads recorded model facts: `root` local, its window 8192 tokens. The capture ran them as mocks (its own admission line says "suite coding: no member holds memory on this machine"), while the recorded list describes llama.cpp backends of the same names: two recordings of the CLI, from two configs, side by side. Naming them after the old frame's models (`qwen3-0.6b`, `gpt-oss-20b`) would have needed facts nobody recorded.
- **The events 1v's scenarios draw are the quiet line until 1v:** `question`, `sources`, `memory`, `todo` and `error` fold into "apogee said something this app does not know yet: <type>", their states kept (a question still makes the turn wait, an error still ends it). None of this part's streams carries one.
- **The private session's notice is drawn under its empty state.** `private-unsent.jsonl` says "private: nothing of this chat is written -- no transcript, no recall" before any turn; the Mac app would draw the conversation for it, the old frame the empty state. The empty state stands while no turn is sent, with the launch's notices under it, so every line the CLI said is drawn.
- **A turn written ahead is drawn before its own turn's first event,** after the launch's notices, as the binary reads its input once it has said them; one the child never reads is not drawn (1v's Not sent draws it). So `chat:play` draws "attaching" before the person's line, as the CLI prints it.
- **1d's replay no longer logs a send to a child that refused its launch** (a non-zero exit, and the `config-typo` mode) as a turn past its recording: such a child exits without reading its input, so no recording is owed. Found by the per-preset test (`chat:play` at `config-typo`); `MachineSession.test.ts` holds it, test first.
- **The model's tag and where-line come from its kind as `models list` types it:** "API · billed per use" and "claude answers through the anthropic API, billed per use." (the backend type, as the CLI writes it); a backend of no kind the design draws (the mock) has no glyph and no tag, and is listed in the model menu under the CLI's own group word, `OTHER`. A menu row's sub line is the row's model and state as `models list` writes them; nothing is disabled (the Mac's settings sheet disables none).
- **Thinking reads `default` until the person picks a mode,** since the session sets none and reports none; the old frame's `auto` was its own state. The menu's footer is the Mac app's for a session that takes `set` ("Applied from the next turn; apogee says each change."), not the old words about restarting, which describe a build without `set`.
- **The answer's footer is the model its result names and the tokens it reports;** the old footer's time (`09:40`) is dropped, since no result carries one.
- **The title is "New session" or "Private session" until 1g names the session from `chats list`;** `chat:play`'s old title ("The board's minutes, summarized") is the list's data.
- **Switch relaunches a session with no turn sent** under `--suite` (and `--force` where forced); with a turn sent it stays off, since moving a conversation restarts its child (`--resume`), which is 1v's Restarting. No suite launch is recorded, and the stand-in matches by prefix, so on `chat:new` and `chat:suite` the relaunch is answered by `new-unsent.jsonl` (on `chat:private`, by `private-unsent.jsonl`): the header's suite words come from the launch's flag, while its model is the recording's `claude`. A known difference until a suite launch is recorded (1v); corrected 2026-11-19 after review, where this entry said such a relaunch could not start.
- **The suites are read by the app root,** as the Mac's library reads them (`config get suites`, `models.default_suite`, each `models status --suite`); a failed `config get suites` is said in the toast in the CLI's words, never drawn as "no suites configured". `chat:suite` opens on `vast`, the recorded suite the CLI prices over this machine's memory, as the old frame opened on `big`. Every chat preset carries these reads (2026-11-19, after review), so Choose a suite, the header's suite and `/suite` open the chooser on `chat:new`, `chat:private`, `chat:play` and `chat:suite` alike; before, all but `chat:suite` said "no recorded answer for apogee config get suites" in the toast.
- **`symphonies list` is a third read for `chat:play`,** so `/play ` offers the CLI's symphonies (the recorded list's 9), as the Mac's library hands `plays` to an execute session.
- **The attach menu attaches the old menu's six samples** (its footer says "In this prototype each item attaches a sample."), each sent as an `attach` line and drawn as a chip of the session's attachments, removable where the session takes `detach` (the Mac's model); the old chips' invented meta (`214 files · .py .json .md`) is not drawn. With the `@` listing, these are the session's two lists that are not the CLI's.
- **A slash command the window does not act on is said in the toast, in the old prototype's words** ("— noted; this prototype doesn't act on it", "isn't a command — type / to see them"), never as a transcript line; those it acts on map onto a line or a door (`/model`, `/think`, `/suite`, `/models`, `/attach`, `/detach`, `/compact`, `/system`, `/temperature`, `/symphonies`, `/help`, and `/play` sent as the turn).
- **The header's menus raise the shell's scrim** (the shell's `menu` is `session` while one is open), so a click anywhere else closes them, as the old global scrim did.
- **The pending line counts its seconds on the prototype's clock** (0.4 s a tick), as the old one did; the time a turn takes is not the CLI's data, and the Mac draws none.
- **`MarkdownDocument` and `CodeLexer` are plain objects, not classes,** so the previews rule does not read the parser as a component.
- **1d's staleness tests named `mock-actor.jsonl` as a recording no source names;** the session's previews name it now, so those two tests name `question-cancelled.jsonl`.
- **The smoke test's toast lookups filter by their words,** since the composer's status line is also `role="status"`, as in the old markup.
- **The document's own decisions were recorded here, not in it:** it is deleted in the same pass.

Decisions from the review's fixes (2026-11-19, the same run), each for veto:
- **The header names the suite a new or private chat runs under, read when it opens.** The app root reads `config get models.default_suite` once a chat that is neither resumed nor `execute` is open, and hands it in (`knowDefaultSuite`, a redraw), so `chat:new` and `chat:private` say "suite fast, the default", the CLI's recorded default, as the old header always named one ("suite coding, the default"). The Mac reads the default only at the chooser and at the execute door, so its header names no suite there until the chooser has opened once: a named difference of when it reads.
- **The row the chooser marks "now" is the suite the header names.** `runningSuite` reads the same facts as `suiteWords`: the CLI's word, else the launch's flag, else the default a new chat runs under. So `chat:suite`'s "fast (the default)" carries the tag, and choosing it leaves Switch off, as the old frame tags its default. The Mac's `runningSuite` ignores the default (it marks only the suite the CLI said, or the launch's flag); a resumed chat the CLI has not spoken of stays unmarked in both.
- **A session nobody launched offers nothing that needs one** (the monitor's, until 1v launches Streaming there). Its empty state draws Attach a folder, Choose a model and Choose a suite off and shows no Private session instead (`face().empty.launched`), the header's suite is off, and the composer's attach is off, so no act reaches an unanswered call, a menu the app never filled, or a line held for good. Before, Private session instead launched a chat no rule answered, Choose a suite said "no recorded answer for apogee config get suites", Choose a model said "apogee has not listed its models.", and Attach a folder was held with no session to take it. The header's model button still opens its menu there, which says the models are not listed.
- **An attachment's chip draws its kind's glyph, read from the path:** a trailing `/` a folder, `.pdf` a PDF, the CLI's own image, audio and video extensions (`media.cpp`'s `medium_of`, lowercased as it reads them), else a file (`AttachmentKind.of`). The six glyphs are the old chips' own (`main.html:731-736` at `b375fac9`); the old chips carried their kind as invented data, so here it is the CLI's rule over the path. Before, every chip but a folder drew the file glyph.
- **A leave carried out opens the session on Sessions, any overlay closed,** as the old `sessionGo` does (`screen: 'chat', overlay: null`), whichever place asked, so 1p's "Open a chat under it" shows the session it opens. A private leave stays put while the `endPrivate` confirm asks.
- **`chat:suite` carries `chat:new`'s private rule,** so Private session instead, the chooser cancelled, replays `private-unsent.jsonl` with the CLI's private notice, as on `chat:new`; before, the prefix rule answered it with `new-unsent.jsonl`.
- **A sent turn reads `working` until the CLI's first event,** on the pending line and in the composer's status: the Mac's state (`Transcript.userSent` sets `.working`; its header says "Working"). The old pending line said `thinking`, or `answering` with thinking off, taken from its own thinking setting, which no event backs. It reads `thinking` once the CLI's `thinking` event arrives. None of the four presets records a typed turn, so in the built file a send stays at `working` (1v's scenarios answer turns).

**Side by side with the frozen old prototype** (1c's `chat_new`, `chat_private`, `chat_play` and `chat_suite` at `installed`, against Playwright shots of the built file at 1440 × 900, still; the main pane only, per the ruling above). Every region, control and word of the old main pane is drawn. Every difference, each intended (the CLI's data, or a later item's), none a defect:
- **`chat:new`:** the model is `claude`, "API · billed per use", where the old frame had `gpt-oss-20b`, local (`new-unsent.jsonl` names `claude`; `models list` types it `anthropic`); the meter is "unknown", dashed (`models info claude` states no window), where the old said `0 / 32.8K 0%`; thinking says `default`, not `auto`; the header says "suite fast, the default" (the CLI's recorded default, read when the chat opens; the session event, before the CLI's M29, names none), where the old said coding.
- **`chat:private`:** the same model, meter, thinking and suite differences ("suite fast, the default", where the old said coding); the CLI's notice is drawn under the empty state.
- **`chat:play`:** the banner is identical ("execute · suite coding · orchestrating · in ingest", the CLI's suite and orchestrating, and the folder the launch names); the persona, project context, "attaching . (2 files, 95 bytes)" and graph lines are word for word the old ones; "attaching" stands before the person's line (the CLI's order); two lines the old frame lacks are the CLI's ("suite coding: no member holds memory on this machine", and "attached .: 2 files, 2 chunks, …"); the stages say `helper` and `root` where the old said `qwen3-0.6b` and `gpt-oss-20b`, their truncation ("… Keep…", "… The board…") the CLI's own and the old's; the answer is word for word; its footer says `● root` with no time; the header says `root`, local, and "not reported" for the meter (a window of 8192, no usage); the title is "New session".
- **`chat:suite`:** the chooser lists the recorded suites (cloud, fast the default, pair, shared, vast) with the CLI's verdicts, open on `vast` ("it does not fit") where the old listed coding, research and big; the toggle, the CLI's total in the error color, the footer, Cancel and Switch (off until forced) are the old ones; "fast (the default)" is marked "now", as the old frame marks its default (where the Mac differs: it marks only the suite the CLI said, or the launch's flag); the subtitle and header say "suite fast, the default".
- **Every chat preset in the `missing` and `config-typo` modes** draws "Apogee isn't installed" or the CLI's refusal at launch, where the old frames ignored the mode.
- **Behind the monitor** the main pane is the empty state, where the old frame drew the TaskGroup session streaming (1v's Streaming); with no session launched, its Attach a folder, Choose a model and Choose a suite are drawn off and Private session instead is not offered (until 1v).
- **A sent turn reads `working` until the CLI's first event,** on the pending line and in the composer's status, where the old said `thinking` (or `answering` with thinking off) from its own setting; it reads `thinking` once the CLI's `thinking` event arrives (the decision above).
- **Switch on `chat:new`, `chat:private` or `chat:suite` relaunches into the launch's own recording** (the stand-in matches by prefix and no suite launch is recorded), so the relaunched header takes its suite from the flag and its model from the recording, until a suite launch is recorded (1v).
- **The inspector's column** is the placeholder (1g), and the sidebar's session list is 1g's.

**Verified.**
- **Test first.** The tests were watched failing before the code that passes them: the play narration, the transcript (on its missing module, then the two-turns-ahead test on the flush-all code, and a mis-indexed stopped-turn test of its own), the session actions, the business reads (`Models`, `SuiteFootprint`, `Symphonies`), the lexer, the markdown parser and its view, the styles front, the capture script (and its own path mistake: a child's working folder is the resolved temp path), the composition's 9 new tests (the last, a replaced session never launched still holding its clock listener, found in review and watched failing on the old line) and the per-preset walk's four presets, the placeholder list, the refused-launch send, and the handed-in facts' redraw. The view-model's 20 first tests were written against a draft set aside: run on 1e's stub they failed 20 of 20, then passed on the draft. The 7 new and 3 changed Playwright tests were run against 1e's committed build, where 9 failed (the 7 new, the double-click and the places tests) and the other 6 passed (the 5 untouched and the theme test, whose toast filter holds on 1e's single status). Written after their code, and passing at once: the face's footer-and-usage test, the layering plants (they pin 1b's rules on the session) and the token scan's code-color plant.

### 2026-11-19 — `session-scenarios-and-states` (backlog item 1v): the session's scenarios and states

**Why.** Measured 2026-11-19 at the frozen revision `b375fac9`: the old session screen's five scenarios (Streaming, Permission, Sources, Error, Question) were hand-written markup, `chat.isTaskgroup` … `isPr` at `main.html:275-578`, their motion counters over strings (`tick()` revealing a hardcoded `REASON` by `progress`, `main.html:2861-2870`, `4947`), and the session's states (Restarting, Not sent, Session ended, the held lines, a saved chat's compaction) three more counters (`lifeBy`, `heldBy`, `compacting`). 1v is the second half of the session screen (1f, split before the run into 1u and 1v): every one of those transcripts now replays a recorded or hand-made stream through 1d's stand-in, extended with five replay rules, and `chat` is the file's default start again.

- [x] **The stand-in's replay rules** (`source/infrastructure/process/StandInChild.ts`, `source/business/session/MachineSession.ts`, `source/business/jobs/SupervisedJob.ts`), each a rule's `Answer` field, each tested against a recording:
  - `freeze` -- a named event (`{ name, event, nth }`, the `nth` line of a type counted from the recording's start): a still frame stands right after it, however many start ticks it has and after `advance(150)`; a live clock plays straight to it, then on at its pace;
  - `exitAfter` -- ticks a chat's child takes to exit once its input closes (what it had written still drains, one line a tick; it takes no line meanwhile), and ticks a job's `.ended` is held back past its next tick; 0, 1d's, unless named;
  - a restart is the rule's next answer (1d's answer list), now replayed by a second launch;
  - `continuations` -- a choice point (`{ after, nth?, by }`): once the `nth` line of type `after` has played, the first answer, turn or setting written picks the branch `by` names it (an answer by its text, a turn as `user`, a setting by what it sets: `set model=claude`, `set temperature=0.4`); the branch is a whole recording sharing the main one's lines to there, played from the line reached; a choice no branch names is noted in `unanswered`;
  - `cancelled` -- Stop: a recording of the same session whose turn a `cancel` ended. A cancel written while a turn is open drops that turn's unplayed lines and plays this recording's ending of the same turn in their place -- its `result` with `finish_reason: "cancelled"` and the calls reported `failed` just before it -- unless the recording itself ends the turn right there (its `result` next, past only `tool_call` lines, as `question-cancelled.jsonl` does), which then plays; a cancel nothing recorded is noted (`chats: cancel during turn 1 of …`), and one with no turn open is ignored, as the binary ignores it. A cancel that ends a turn at the choice point spends it.
- [x] **Data and Business:** `ProtocolEvent.recalled` (a `memory` event's named past chats, 52a's `sessions`; `RecalledChat`) and `business/reads/SavedChat.ts` (`SavedChat`, `SavedTurn`, `SavedAttachment`, `SavedToolCall`, `SavedWarning`: `chats show` read back, the Mac app's names).
- [x] **The session module** (`source/presentation/session/`), the Mac app's names:

  - `ViewModels/SessionViewModel.ts` gains the child's life (`restart()`, a stopped child handing the conversation to the next with `--resume`, Start again through `start()`, which resumes the conversation its child saved whether or not it was launched as a resume), the held lines' rules (`sendOrHold` behind lines still held, released once a child takes them, a suite move last; `heldWords`, `heldHelp`, the settings footer by state), Not sent and `resend`/`canResend`, the cards' `answer` (one answer line per item) and `skip`, `preselect` (the option a card opens with picked, a preset's), `retry` and `askInstead` (the Error card's fixes), the citations (`openCitation`, `citeTurn`, `closeCitation`, `openSource`), the suite move after a turn (`moveSuite`, the Mac's `moveWords` where the CLI says nothing of it), its `reader` (the Mac's `init(…reader:)`: a `CommandRunning`, the sixth constructor argument, read with `chats show <id>` on the binary it located), and `face()`'s new rows (a question card's every item, a denial's note, a turn's footer under its last answer alone, a failed load named by the model that failed), the held strip and the composer's life words;

The recipes `chat-permission-yes`, `-session`, `-always` and `-no` (`chat-permission-*.jsonl`, 84, 84, 84 and 91 lines: two `read_file`s and `write_file` behind the gate in a folder seeded with `config.toml` and a 96-line `src/ingest/runner.py`, the CLI's own diff in the question's `detail`); `chat-sources` (`chat-sources.jsonl`, 97 lines: `set` to `claude`, a 300-page `storage-engine-design.pdf` made by `minimalPdf` and a 220-line `postgresql.conf` attached, and the binary's own `sources` event: pages 185–189, lines 209–219 and pages 191–193, lexical scores 0.94, 0.91 and 0.91); `chat-streaming` (`chat-streaming.jsonl`, 276); `chat-question-upload`, `-batch`, `-host`, `-own` and `-skip` (74, 94, 79, 86 and 81: `ask_user` answered each way, Skip as an empty answer); `chat-kv` (`chat-kv.jsonl`, 81, `business/reads/chats-show-kv.json`, and three resumes: `resumed-kv.jsonl`, 1 line; `resumed-kv-pair.jsonl`, 2, under the suite `pair`; `resumed-kv-turn.jsonl`, 26); `chat-error-retry` and `chat-error-switch` (52 and 53, the screenshot attached); and Stop, each by the recipe's `cancel`: `chat-permission-cancel` (44 lines: a cancel at the gate; the binary reports `write_file` `failed`, then the turn's `result` `cancelled`), `chat-question-cancel` (45: a cancel at the question; the `result` `cancelled` carrying the prose streamed before it) and `chat-streaming-cancel` (118: `streaming-root-cancel.json`, the follow-up's reasoning slowed by `delay_ms: 20`, a cancel at turn 2's 20th delta; the `result` `cancelled`, text empty). Not captured: a capture's own resume would name another chat than the kv chat the three presets resume, so the branch could not share the main recording's session line;

Decisions the item recorded before the build:
- 2026-11-14: **The user's call:** the prototype is rebuilt in React, TypeScript and Vite with the production GUIs' four layers and MVVM modules, built into one self-contained `lib/design/main.html`, in v0.2.1.
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** data is the CLI's recorded files read in place; a state nobody recorded gets a hand-made file in the CLI's exact shape, named `*-handmade*`.
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** parity with the frozen old prototype is judged on layout, states, controls and wording, not pixels or data.
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** the old prototype is frozen (fixes only) from 1c's baseline; new design is drawn in the React app now that 1e has shipped.
- 2026-11-14: The parent (1f) was marked **split first** into 1u and this part.
- 2026-11-19: **The split** (the user's answer before the group run, 2026-11-18): this part is the parent's second, gated on 1u; 1g is gated on it.
- 2026-11-19: **The ids** (the run owner's ruling, for veto): 1f stays spent, 1u the first part, this part 1v, both ahead of 1g.

- 2026-11-19: **Streams the mock cannot make are hand-made** (the run owner's ruling, for veto): Streaming's `fetch_url`, its embedding helper line and its recall; Sources' retrieval and its `sources` event; Error's failed load; Question's `run_command`; Ended's child dying mid-answer -- each built from the capture's own lines. (What was hand-made in the end is listed above: Sources and the embedding line were not; decisions below.)
- 2026-11-19: **The freeze, the replayed exit and restart, the continuations and a job's held-back end land here** (the run owner's ruling), each with the first preset that needs it.
- 2026-11-19: **The compact jobs' state is this part's; its busy rows and open row menu are 1g's** (the parent's consumed decision).
- 2026-11-19: **`chat` is the default start again**, ending 1e's interim monitor default.
- 2026-11-19: **What 1u shipped that this part builds on** (1u's hand-off): `Transcript.ts` folds `stopped` and `ended`; the 1v events were the quiet line, their states kept; an end was a quiet line until `EndedCard`; `HeldLine` had no strip; a still preset's sends are `writeAhead`; the monitor's empty state's acts that need a session were off until this part launched Streaming there; a Switch relaunch is answered by prefix.

The open calls' defaults, taken as stated (2026-11-18, the user's answers before the run):
- **Each missing stream recorded with `capture-fixture.sh`'s recipe** (1u's capture script) against the mock backend, from the branch build; hand-made only what the binary cannot produce. **Tried, and the capture went further than the document expected** (critic G6 checked): 14 recipes ran, Permission and Sources need no hand-made line at all, and the rest are each their capture plus the lines listed above.
- **Each scenario's actor file carries the old scenario's words** (the TaskGroup question, its reasoning and both answers; the retry change, its reasoning and diff; the WAL answer, cited; the stack trace answer; the PR 482 review, its question and each reply).
- **A preset freezes its replay at a named event in a still frame**, and a live clock plays to it and on: `chat` and `monitor` stand three-quarters through Streaming's reasoning (the old `progress: 30` of 40), `chat:compacting`'s session at 15 of 40 (its 29th of 78 reasoning deltas, against `chat`'s 59th); `chat:retry` and `chat:pr` stop at their card, as 1d's replay already waits at an unanswered question.
- **Each branch is its own capture from the shared opening turn, or hand-made;** clicking any choice plays its own reply. **Question's "change answer" (the old `reask`) is not drawn once an answer is written**: the CLI reads one answer per question. Until Answer is pressed the choice changes freely.
- **Question's Claude lines are the view's, not stream content:** the session line names `claude`, and `chat:pr` reads `models-list-server-tools.json`, where `claude` is the `claude-cli` subscription; "Claude is asking" is the session's model, capitalized, and "tokens not reported by the Claude app" the footer of a subscription's answer whose result reports no usage. The Mac app says "Apogee is asking" and draws no tokens text there: both differences from the Mac, kept as the prototype's words.
- **The replayed exit and restart:** a chat rule's answer says when its child exits after its input closes, in ticks; a restart is its next answer; held lines go to the next child once its `session` line plays, a suite move last; Not sent is a write refused because the child's input had closed; Session ended is a stream cut mid-answer with a non-zero exit and its stderr tail, in the Mac's `EndedCard` words.
- **The compact jobs' state is a small class in `session/ViewModels/`** (`Compactions`), held by the composition until 1g's library takes it; `chats compact <id> --yes` on `JobStarting`, not streamed; `chat:compacting` starts both jobs in its setup, retry first, each held back 30 ticks past its line (the old 30 ticks and 12-tick offset are not ported).
- **The Error scenario keeps the old card's words and both fixes**, each running what the CLI has: the switch is `set` of the model to the billed backend `models list` names (`claude`), then the turn sent again; Unload and retry has no command (the CLI has no `unload`: searched again 2026-11-19, and a session's model is held by its own child), so it sends the turn again and replays a hand-made continuation. The card's words are the CLI's where they differ (below).
- **The kv answer's headings and table are 1u's markdown component's;** nothing was added to `styles` for a block. (A span was: citations, below.)

Decisions taken while building (2026-11-19, an unattended group run), each for veto:
- **The user's answers before the run:** the npm registry and Playwright's Chromium were allowed; neither was needed (`package.json` and the lock file unchanged). A Playwright test opening the built file from `file://` stood in for your look. **You try the file afterwards** (Not done).
- **The capture script grew what the recipes needed** (`before`, `resumes` with an actor swap, seeded subfolders, `minimalPdf`), each test first against its fake binary. Two capture surprises were kept as the CLI's data: the mock's turns are consumed by the title generation and the follow-up's utility rewrite, so `streaming-root.json` carries a step for each (the title is the old session's, "TaskGroup timeouts in the ingest runner"); and the attached folder's retrieval gave Streaming a real `sources` event in each turn.
- **No embedding line, no context-budget notice, no "earlier: 4 exchanges" in Streaming:** the capture's own helper line ("utility — rewriting the follow-up into a search query with root") stands where the old embedding line stood, since the capture's notice says no embedding model is set and the mock's window is unknown (so nothing was trimmed); the old exchange count was invented, and a resumed chat's earlier turns are read back and drawn instead (the kv chat). Sources likewise retrieves by its words, so its activity has no embedding line.
- **Sources is captured whole:** the CLI's own `pdftotext` read a 300-page PDF made at capture time, so its `sources` event, scores and order are the binary's ([1] pages 185–189, [2] postgresql.conf lines 209–219, [3] pages 191–193). The answer's `[n]` follow that order.
- **Citations are a span of the markdown component** (`citations` on `MarkdownText`), since the CLI's answer carries `[n]` in its prose; the open citation's card is drawn under the answer (the old draws it under the paragraph), says the file, the place and the CLI's score, and has no quoted excerpt (the `sources` event carries none). "Open at page n" says the old prototype's toast, "Opens the source in Preview at that page (prototype)".
- **A turn's sources are drawn after its answer**, as the old SOURCES list is, and its recall at the head of its reply, as the old recall line stands above the reasoning; the reducer looks past the sources and recall rows when it continues a turn's activity, so the retrieval and recall the CLI says between its query rewrite and its reasoning do not split the activity in two (the Mac draws each row where it arrives; the transcript keeps the CLI's order, and the view moves the recall).
- **A switch to a model billed per use is the CLI's notice, marked:** "switched to claude -- from the next turn" with the API glyph and " · billed per use from here" where `models list` types the model `api` (the design's rule; the only place the notice's text is read). An API answer's footer carries the API glyph in place of its kind's dot, as the old one does.
- **The failed-load card:** title "<model> didn’t load" where the CLI's message says it could not load the model (`<backend>: could not load the model at '…'`, its `ProviderError` prefix allowed), the model the one the session ran on when the error came (it still names vast after "Ask claude instead"), its body the message's first line, prefix and all, its details the whole message; "Unload and retry" names no model (the CLI names none holding memory, and a one-shot `system` holds none); "Ask claude instead · billed" names the first `api` chat backend `models list` lists. Any other failed turn is the Mac's card, The turn failed and Try again.
- **A retry draws the turn sent again** as the person's line (the Mac's `retry`), and "Ask … instead" sends the `set` then the turn: the CLI's switch notice arrives after the drawn line.
- **The permission card's words:** "Change a file?" by its tool (the Mac's labels), "<model> wants to write to an existing file" (a new file where the CLI's diff starts `--- /dev/null`), the target as the CLI's gate names it (relative to the root), its `+`/`−` counts read from the diff; answered, the Mac's quiet line ("write_file src/ingest/runner.py — denied by you"), and under a denial the old note, "you denied write_file — root was told and carried on without it" (`main.html:424` at `b375fac9`, the model the session's).
- **While the permission gate waits, its turn's activity stays open, "paused · waiting for you"** (the old `thinkOpen = rtWaiting`); a question leaves it closed, as the old Question frame does. **No pending line stands beside a card, or under a live activity:** each already says the turn is at work.
- **Skip writes an empty answer** (one per item), which the capture recorded as its own branch; the card says "skipped".
- **The free-text branch is one recorded answer** ("Per endpoint, shared by every upload to it"); other words of one's own are noted in `unanswered`, and the turn waits as an unrecorded turn does.
- **A closed child's written lines drain while it exits:** the stand-in plays what the child had written, one a tick, during its exit delay, so `chat:restarting`'s first child, stopped before the clock started, still says its launch lines.
- **`chat:restarting` moves the kv chat to the suite `pair`** (the old `big`): the move is said in the Mac's `moveWords`, since no child had said its vocabulary when it was made; a child being stopped says nothing of the suite (its late `session` line leaves the move as it was), so the header says "suite pair".
- **`chat:notsent` restarts the kv chat and writes its turn while the stop has closed the child's input** (the Mac's 6a test: a stop closed its input first); a refused turn written before the child spoke is drawn after its launch lines, once it is going.
- **A line written before the child has said what it reads is held, not refused:** `set` and `compact` wait while `accepts` is unknown (the Mac would restart on an older build); and a turn written ahead counts as a turn in flight for the held lines, so `chat:ended`'s setting and compact are held for the turn its child dies in.
- **The resumed chat's earlier turns are read by the session itself**, through its `reader` -- the Mac's `init(starter:launch:reader:)`, a `CommandRunning` handed in as its sixth constructor argument (the composition's reads; a preview's, a `ReadRunner` over its own child) -- with `chats show <id>` on the binary it located, before its first child opens and on Start again. (The first build set a `readEarlier` closure after construction; fixed after review.)
- **The presets name what their setups need in their own rules:** the kv chat's id is its chat rule's `--resume`, the compactions are its jobs' `chats compact <id>`, so no view-model imports a recording; the scenarios' turns are `scenarioTurns` beside 1u's `playLine` (and the previews' `turns`).
- **The face says absence explicitly** (an empty string, `0`, `shown: false`) so the per-preset walk's undefined check still holds every new row.
- **The Unload comments** (`MonitorViewModel.ts`, `AppViewModel.ts`) name 1g, 1k, 1l and 1m: 1u and 1v hold nothing a row unloads.
- **`.` and `..` attach as folders** (the Streaming capture attaches `.`).
- **The document's own decisions were recorded here, not in it:** it is deleted in the same pass.

Decisions taken while fixing the review's findings (2026-11-19, the same unattended run), each for veto:
- **A setting is named at a choice point by what it sets** (`set model=claude`, `set temperature=0.4`, `set think=on`: each field `key=value` in the line's order, a text unquoted), so only the recorded switch picks `chat:crash`'s switch branch. Two reviews asked for this with different rules for a setting that names no branch; the document's guardrail decided it: "a line no continuation names is noted in the child's `unanswered`". So a temperature, a thinking mode or another model after the error is noted (`chats: set temperature=0.4 after the error of apogee chat …`), the choice point is spent, and the replay plays on as recorded -- a retry after it is a turn past the recording, noted and left working, never answered by a reply recorded at other settings. (The other review's rule, a setting that moves no model leaving the choice point open for the retry, would play vast's recorded retry at a temperature nobody recorded.)
- **Stop is a replay rule of its own, `cancelled`, not a continuation:** a cancel can come at any line of a turn (mid-reasoning on Streaming), where a branch played "from the line reached" would have to share the main recording to that line. So a cancel drops the open turn's unplayed lines and plays the cancel capture's ending of the same turn -- the `result` `cancelled` and the calls reported `failed` before it -- which keeps each cancel stream a pure capture (`chat:pr`'s and Streaming's main streams are hand-made, their captures are not). A cancel that ends a turn at a choice point spends it, so a turn sent after is noted once.
- **A stopped turn draws the Mac's `stopped` line,** as the old prototype draws it under a card the stop closed ("write_file src/ingest/runner.py — not answered: the turn ended first", then "stopped"); on Streaming the old note said "stopped by you while thinking — what it had so far is kept" and the activity "thinking stopped at …s · you stopped it". Not ported: the CLI rolls a cancelled turn back ("the cancelled line and anything said for it never reach the saved conversation", machine-mode.md), so "kept" would not be honest, and no event carries the seconds.
- **`chat:pr` opens with Per batch picked,** as the old frame's state does (`pr: { choice: 'batch' }`): `preselect`, a pick on the card only, nothing written until Answer (the first build opened it unpicked, the Mac's).
- **A question of several items** draws a fieldset per item and writes one answer line per item, Answer on once each has one (the Mac's `QuestionCardView`); no preset's stream asks two, so `chat-question-two-handmade.jsonl` draws it for a preview and a test.
- **A turn's footer stands under its last answer alone** (`chat:pr`'s prose before its question had one too).
- **`chat:notsent`'s Send again is answered** by the returned child's `resumed-kv-turn.jsonl`, the chat-kv recipe's own capture of that turn (its full answer keeps the actor's "or"; the old frame draws none of it).
- **`chat:ended`'s Start again** sends the held setting and compact to the next child, which answers each (`resumed-kv-held-handmade.jsonl`, keyed `set temperature=0.4`; the compact written after it is no choice).
- **Start again resumes the conversation its child saved,** whether or not the session was launched as a resume: the saved id is read before the transcript that names it is replaced (the Mac's `launch.resume = savedChatID`).

The frames of `chat`, `chat:pr`, `chat:ended`, `chat:crash` and `chat:retry` were shot again after the review's fixes. The differences that remain, each intended:

- **`chat:crash`:** the attach notices are the CLI's (the screenshot "described by vast, from another chat's index"); the card's body is the CLI's message ("vast: could not load the model at '…'", its prefix kept), "Unload and retry" names no model, the switch names `claude`.

### 2026-11-19 — `sessions-inspector-execute-and-console` (backlog item 1g): the session list, the inspector, the execute door and the console

**Why.** Measured at the frozen revision `b375fac9` (8,082 lines): the old sidebar's session list (`chatDefs()`, 13 chats shaped `{id, title, group, time, model, ctx}`), the inspector beside the session (`ins`, its turn rows a table per scenario), the "New execute session" door (`ex`, its `exCanOrch` always true) and the console (`con`, its three refusals hard-coded) were the last of the Sessions place still drawn as invented data; 1u reserved the inspector's column with a placeholder and said in the toast that a listed chat and the door were this item's. The CLI's `chats list` row is `{id, updated, turns, name, backend, model, origin}`. 1g ports the four onto the Mac app's `sessions` and `console` modules, over the stand-in, and the list shows on every screen.

- [x] **`business/reads/Chats.ts`** (the Mac's `Chats.swift`): `ChatSummary` (`chats list`'s rows, `updated` kept as written), `ChatDetails` (`chats info`'s `chat.info` document and the sheet's fields under the text's own labels) and `RecallListing` (`chats recall`'s summaries and its count line).

Decisions the item recorded before the build:
- 2026-11-14: **The user's call:** the prototype is rebuilt in React, TypeScript and Vite with the production GUIs' four layers and MVVM modules, so it stays the design's cross-platform source of truth.
- 2026-11-14: **The user's call:** the build is one self-contained HTML file that opens by double-click; the canvas becomes view-only and is retired at the end, so the `execute` and `console` boards are reviewed in the built file.
- 2026-11-14: **The user's call:** the design has its own queue; this item is in v0.2.1, track 1.
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** data is the Mac app's recorded fixtures, read in place, with `*-handmade*` files for unrecorded states; view-models are plain TypeScript that never imports React.
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** parity is judged on layout, states, controls and wording, not pixels or data, against the old prototype frozen at 1c (fixes only).
- 2026-11-14: Item 1g of track 1, gated on 1f because the inspector reads the session's state.
- 2026-11-19: 1f was split into 1u and 1v, and 1f stays spent (the user's answer before the group run: "1g's gate moves to the second"); this item was gated on 1v. The header's inspector button and the `endPrivate` confirm are 1u's, the `chat:*` session states 1v's; 1u reserved the inspector's 304 px with a placeholder, which this item replaces; 1u and 1v compared only the main pane, so the session list and the inspector of the 13 `chat:*` presets are compared here (the run owner's ruling, for veto).
- 2026-11-19: **Where 1u's interim pieces were** (1u's hand-off): the placeholder in `SessionExtras.tsx`; the model facts' reads in `AppViewModel.readModelFacts` and `observe`; the suite reads in `AppViewModel.chooseSuite` and `openSession`; the leave intent in `AppViewModel.carryOut`. Each moved into `SessionLibraryViewModel`.
- 2026-11-19: **What 1v shipped that this part builds on** (1v's hand-off): the nine presets and `AppViewModel.applyStart`; `streamingRules('chat' | 'start')` and `openStreaming()`, which `console` reuses at `'start'`; `Compactions`; the session's `reader`; Stop's `cancelled` endings; `leave({ type: 'open', id })` from a recalled chat; the three presets that close the inspector; the session's `face()`.
- (consumed) 1d: where hand-made files live, the fixed "now", the stand-in's `command` and `__complete`. 1e: the places half of the sidebar, the keys, the sheet and confirm host, the toast. 1u: the inspector button and its `inspectorOpen`, the `endPrivate` confirm (the list's rows and new-session buttons call 1u's `leave`). 1v: the session states, the compact jobs behind `chat:compacting`, and the Streaming session `console` opens over at its starting frame.

The open calls' defaults, taken as stated (2026-11-18, the user's answers before the run):
- **One `SessionLibraryViewModel`, as in the Mac.** It passed 800 lines, so its parts went into their own files under the Mac's names (`ChatGroup`, `ExecuteForm`, `Inspector`, `InspectorTurn`, `SessionKeys`, and the faces' types in `LibraryFaces`); the view-model itself is 866 lines.
- **`SessionsScreen` is the Sessions place's screen**, 1u's `SessionWindow` with the inspector beside it; the 13 `chat:*` presets render through it, and every 1u and 1v test stays green (two of their expectations changed, below).
- **The date groups come from each row's recorded `updated`, against the stand-in's fixed now** (the Mac's `when(_:)`), and the glyph from the backend's kind (`kind(of:)`).
- **The inspector's turn rows come from the replayed session events** (`InspectorTurn`), its model facts from `models info`, its helpers from `models status`.
- **The console asks the stand-in for completions through `__complete`.** On every preset, a line with a recorded answer prints it (`models list` as text, hand-made; `config scan`, the CLI's `config-scan.txt`; a job the preset itself recorded) and any other line prints the prototype's "Done.": the console's answers (`consoleRules`) carry a catch-all, an empty run exiting 0, and every preset carries them (`consoling`). They answer on the console's own stand-in child, beside the preset's rules, so the catch-all answers only a line typed in the console (a decision below, for veto).
- **The console's refusals are the prototype's three, in its words, in a small table** (`refusals` in `ConsoleViewModel.ts`); the Mac's parity table is not ported.
- **The inspector is drawn at the prototype's 304 px**: `flex: 0 0 304px` with its 1 px border, content-box, as the old (305 px laid out); the 360 in BuildLayout and `tokens.json`'s `layoutPx.inspector` goes on 1s's list.

Decisions taken while building (2026-11-19, an unattended group run), each for veto:
- **The user's answers before the run:** the npm registry and Playwright's Chromium were allowed; neither was needed (`package.json` and the lock file unchanged; `~/Library/Caches/ms-playwright` still holds `chromium-1248`, `chromium_headless_shell-1248` and `ffmpeg-1013`). A Playwright test opening the built file from `file://` stood in for your look. **You try the file afterwards** (Not done). No capture was needed: no `apogee` binary ran.
- **⌥⌘I is a toggle** (the readiness note's reading of "⌥⌘I opens the inspector"): the old prototype starts with the inspector open on 10 of the 13 `chat:*` presets, so the acceptance test presses ⌥⌘I until it is closed, then once to open it.
- **The library is shell-free, as the Mac's is:** it speaks through closures the composition wires (`say`, `present`, `dismissSheet`, `ask`, `showSessions`, `goToSessions`, `openModels`, `menuChanged`), the composition tells it the place shown (`showing`), and the shell hands it every key it does not own (`placeKeys`).
- **The inspector stays as it was across sessions** (the old global `s.inspector`, the Mac's library-level `inspectorOpen`); it stays on the session (1u's consumed `inspectorOpen`), and the library carries it to the next. The three presets that close it set it before their session opens.
- **The inspector's reads are made once a session, once the session has said its suite** (the Mac reads them as the pane appears): before its `session` event the suite is only the default a new chat would run under. The Streaming captures' session lines say they run under no suite (their throwaway config had none), so their roles are read with `--suite off`, which no recording answers: `models-status-off-handmade.json` (above). `refreshRoles` reads again when the suite moves.
- **Reads are documents:** `chats info` and `chats recall` are asked with `--output-format json`, the recorded binary's (M31) form; the Mac's text fallback for older binaries is not ported (no `__machine-schema` here).
- **Read back, don't mirror, on a stand-in that keeps no state:** Rename, Delete, Forget and a compaction run the CLI's verbs and read the list (or recall) back, and say what the CLI did in the toast ("Renamed to …", "Deleted …", the CLI's forget line); the stand-in answers the next read with the same recording, so a renamed row keeps its recorded name and a deleted one stays listed. A stateful stand-in is no item's yet.
- **The toasts are the Mac's where the old said none** ("Deleted <name>"), the old's where it did ("Renamed to <name>", "Compacting <name> in its session", the CLI's forget line).
- **Opening a listed chat resumes it, as the Mac does** (`chat --resume <id>`, its turns read back with `chats show`); only the kv chat has a recorded resume (on its three presets), so another row's session ends at launch in the stand-in's words, "no recorded answer for apogee chat … --resume <id>".
- **The date groups are UTC calendar days**, the zone of the stand-in's fixed now, so a row never moves with the viewer's zone; the time is the old 24-hour `14:03`. Two Today rows (14:03, 13:40) stand after the fixed noon, kept as the old list shows them; nothing on screen says "now".
- **The list's rows name the CLI's backend** in their tooltip (`<name> · <backend>`), and a backend the config types as none of the three keeps the glyph's place (an empty 12 px), so every title starts in line (found in comparison, below).
- **The `pr` chat's row is on `claude`, its session's model**, which the shared config types as Anthropic's API: its glyph is the API cylinder where the old drew the subscription arrows; `pg`, on the hand-made `claude-app`, carries the subscription glyph. On `chat:pr`, whose recorded config (`models-list-server-tools.json`) types `claude` as the subscription and lists no local backend, `pr` and `wal` draw the subscription glyph and the local rows none (their place kept): the CLI's data in two recorded configs, side by side.
- **The THIS TURN rows, from the events:** Thinking (the setting asked for, `default`), each helper or stage line (`Utility`, `Stage 1/2`), Memory (the chats recalled), Search (the files a `sources` event names, and its excerpts), each tool call (its target, its state), the gate's call (`+11 −3 · needs your OK`, the old row's words), a question (its first item's header, `waiting for you` or `→ <answer>`), a failed load (the model in the error color, the CLI's words), and Usage (`reported when the turn ends`, the tokens a result reports, or `not reported`; none after a failed turn). No time column: no event carries one.
- **CONTEXT, with no usage reported anywhere:** "unknown" in the heading, the window from `models info` (`8,192-token window`, or `window not reported`), and words for why: a subscription's are the old's ("The Claude app doesn’t share token counts"); any other's are new, "Not reported. No answer has reported its token counts yet, so Apogee can’t tell how full the window is." Its lines are the session's attachments and the chats it recalled.
- **MODEL's facts, from `models info`, under the old labels where the CLI states the fact:** local -- Format (the list's group), Runs on (`llamacpp on this Mac`), Size, Window; API -- Runs on, Billing (`per use`), Key (`provider`), Window; subscription -- Runs on, Billing, Usage, Window (`not reported`).
- **HELPERS lists the five helper roles in the old order** (Embedding, Vision, Transcription, Utility, Extraction), each in the CLI's words; "n set · m fall back" counts the ones not `(unset …`. Change helpers and Open in library go to Models (1h's tabs).
- **RECALL adds the Mac's summaries** (`RecallSummaries`: each name and date, its text on a click) under the old count line; Forget asks in the old words ("Now: recall holds 8 chat summaries in …").
- **The confirms are destructive in the old's error color**, as every old confirm's default `runBg` is.
- **The door's Choose… is inert, as the old one is** (a browser picks no folder for the CLI); the folder is the preset's.
- **The console's Run and input stay enabled while a run is live, as the old ones are;** `run()` refuses a second run, as the Mac's does.
- **An empty line of output keeps its height** (the CLI prints one between "Local backends" and "SafeTensors"; the old drew it zero high).
- **The private row keeps the old "deleted on close"** although `--private` writes nothing at all.
- **The `console` preset starts 10 ticks in**, so `models list`'s seven lines play and its run ends before `reset` runs; `start()` runs the rest once the clock has started.
- **`chat:compacting` opens kv's row menu over the shell's scrim**, as the old `menu: 'row:kv'`.
- **Two 1u expectations changed, each for what 1g now reads:** `chat:new`'s call list (the library's `chats list` and `models list` first, then the launch, the default suite, the roles and recall once the session has spoken), and the Places test's heading under the monitor (the Streaming chat's listed name, "TaskGroup timeouts in the ingest runner", for 1u's "New session"); 1u's 304 px check now measures the old pane's 304 px and its border (305 laid out); 1v's Stop test looks for "stopped" in the conversation, since the inspector's status says it too.
- **The shell's placeholder panel stays** (`PlaceholderPanel`, no overlay uses it now); its previews' words no longer say the console is 1g's.
- **The document's own decisions were recorded here, not in it:** it is deleted in the same pass.

After the review (2026-11-19, the same run), six defects the reviewers confirmed, each fixed test first, each decision for veto:
- **The console answers on every preset, as the default says.** It printed "Done." only at `console`, and there `config scan` printed nothing of `config-scan.txt` (the document lists it among the console's recordings): every other preset said the stand-in's "Didn’t start: no recorded answer for apogee …". Now `consoleRules()` carries `config scan` too, and every preset carries the console's answers (`Presets.ts`'s `consoling`, into a new optional `Preset.console`).
- **The console's catch-all is the console's alone** (the run owner's choice between the reviewers' two, for veto): the console runs over a stand-in child of its own (`AppViewModel.consoleChild`), answering the preset's rules with the console's beside them -- a job the preset recorded beats the console's of the same arguments. The other way, the catch-all merged into every preset's one table, would have had a row's compaction nobody recorded say "Compacted <id>" on every screen, which the CLI never said; here it still says the stand-in's "Not compacted: no recorded answer for apogee chats compact <id> --yes". The per-preset walk holds both children's `unanswered` empty.
- **No row is current off Sessions** (the prototype's rule; the Mac app highlights the open row on every place): the old list's `cur = s.screen === 'chat' && …` and `privateBg`. The open session's row (its selection background, primary text, local chip tint and `aria-current`), a closed group's mark and the private row's background are drawn only while Sessions is shown (`showing`, told on each route change); the private row keeps its primary text, as the old markup does. Every later place's preset that keeps a session open behind it (1h's Models presets first) then matches the old frames' list.
- **A row's act leaves the console and the monitor open**, as the old `act` sets only `{ screen: 'chat', menu: null }`: Details…, Rename…, Compact… and Delete… show Sessions with the overlay as it was (`goToSessions`), the sheet or confirm over it; Stop compacting only says "Not compacted: stopped", on the place shown, as the old `stopCompacting`. A leave (a new, private or listed chat, the door's Open) still closes any overlay, as the old `sessionGo`.
- **"Summarize older turns now" waits for a known context,** as the old `cantCompact`'s `!ctxKnown`: off while the window or the tokens are unknown (claude, whose `models info` states no window, even after a result reporting 4,000 tokens or more), under 4,000 tokens, or once the session has ended.
- **THIS TURN starts afresh when a child numbers its turns afresh:** a `session` line whose `next_turn` is at or below the turn held drops it -- a private conversation started again numbers from 1, and `chat:ended`'s Start again resumes the kv chat at 2 again, its dead turn unsaved -- while a resumed child that numbers on keeps it until its own turn speaks.

**Side by side with the frozen old prototype** (1c's 13 `chat_*`, `execute` and `console` frames at `installed`, against Playwright shots of the built file at 1440 × 900, still, the sidebar and the inspector cropped side by side). Every region, control, state and word of the old list and inspector is drawn. Defects found and fixed, each test first: the gate's row said "waiting for you" (the old "needs your OK"); a failed load's row said "vast didn’t load" with a Usage row under it (the old draws the model in the error color and no usage); a play's stage rows were labelled with their whole first words (now `Stage 1/2`); a row whose backend has no kind lost its glyph's place, so its title started out of line. The review found six more (above): off Sessions the open row and the private row stayed current, a row's act closed the console and the monitor, the console said "Done." only at `console` (and printed no `config scan`), the context's compact button was on with an unknown window, and THIS TURN kept a dead child's turn; none changes these 15 frames, each on Sessions with no row act made. The differences that remain, each intended (the CLI's data):
- **The list, everywhere:** the same 13 titles, groups, counts, folds and times; Updated and Id are the CLI's (`2026-11-14T14:03:07Z`, `20261119-111221-123d`, where the old wrote `2026-10-26…` and `20261026-…`); Turns are what each capture answered (Streaming 2 where the old said 6, kv 1 where it said 2); the `pr` row's glyph (above); standup's chip is local, on `scribe`, where the old's was the local MLX Gemma; the tooltips name the backend.
- **The inspector, everywhere:** the turn label from the row's time (`· turn at 13:40` where the old wrote 13:41 for retry); the rows above, with no seconds; CONTEXT "unknown" with the window `models info` states (8,192 where the old drew 32,768, 84%); MODEL's facts from `models info` (the old's gpt-oss-20b, Qwen3-VL-8B and Claude Sonnet are `root`, `vast` and `claude`); HELPERS in the CLI's words ("2 set · 3 fall back" under no suite, where the old said 3 and 2); RECALL's count is 8 where the old said 312, with the summaries under it.
- **`chat` / `chat:compacting` / `console`:** THIS TURN is Thinking, Utility (the rewrite), Memory, Search, `fetch_url docs.python.org`, `read_file src/ingest/runner.py`, Usage, where the old had Embedding, its URL and line range; no "Not sent: 2 earlier exchanges".
- **`chat:retry`:** the two reads' state `done` where the old wrote their line ranges; no "Files read" line.
- **`chat:wal`:** Search names the two files with "3 excerpts" where the old said "12 passages → 3 kept"; no "Sent: 3 passages" line; Usage not reported.
- **`chat:crash`:** the model is `vast` with the CLI's load failure, where the old said "didn't load: needs 7.9 GB, 5.2 GB free".
- **`chat:pr`:** run_command and read_file as the CLI names them; the question's row is its header, "Scope"; the list's glyphs are its config's (above).
- **`chat:new`, `chat:private`, `chat:suite`:** as the old, but for MODEL (`claude`, the API) and CONTEXT.
- **`chat:play` / `execute`:** the turn rows are the play's two stages; the door's suites are the recorded ones and `coding` (cloud, coding, fast, pair, shared, vast, under "The default suite (coding)") where the old listed its own, and its models the configured chat backends where the old listed gpt-oss-20b, qwen3-0.6b and claude-sonnet-5; the note, the folder, Orchestrate and the line are word for word.
- **`chat:restarting`, `chat:notsent`, `chat:ended`:** the inspector closed, as the old; ⌥⌘I opens it on the kv chat's row.
- **`console`:** word for word but for the empty output line's height (above); the strip's console button is pressed (`aria-expanded`).

**Verified.**
- **Test first.** Watched failing before their code: `Chats.test.ts` (on its missing module), `ChatGroup.test.ts` and `ExecuteForm.test.ts` (on theirs), `ConsoleViewModel.test.ts` (9 of 9 against the stub), `SessionLibraryViewModel.test.ts` (18 against the stub; then 2 on the test's own setup, which wrote no Streaming turns), the shell's key hand-off, the composition's 8 new tests and the walk over every preset with the library's and the console's faces (53 failing on `app.library` missing), the inspector's read once a session (failing on a second read the session's suite explained: the fix is the read made once the session has spoken, and two expectations moved with it), the gate's words, the failed load's row and the glyph's place (`SessionViews.test.tsx`). Written after their code and proven by a mutant instead: the stage rows (the mutant failed the test; the code was restored). ### 2026-11-19 — `models-screens` (backlog item 1h): the Models place

**Why.** Measured 2026-11-14 and again at the frozen revision `b375fac9` (8,082 lines): the old Models place -- its four tabs (`models screen`, 5711), Stored weights (5441), the four store sheets (5579), the download sheet (5923), five of the confirm section's kinds (5962) and two of the generic sheets (`adapters`, `pairDrafter`, 7778) -- ran on invented data: `baseModels()` (15 models), `storeSets()` (19 sets) and two camelCase `jobs` ticking off the clock, no `object:` key anywhere. The CLI's pull is a stream of `job`, `stage`, `bytes` and `document` events, and the Mac app's `models` module (14 files, 6,861 lines) reads the CLI's documents. 1h ports the place onto the Mac module's six view-models over the stand-in, with its nine presets, so jobs, stored sets and roles come from the CLI's recordings, and a pull and a conversion land in Stored weights from what their own runs report.

- [x] **The shared job list** (`business/jobs/`): `ChildRegistry.ts`, the Mac's `ChildRegistry` -- every child the app runs, registered by its supervisor and gone at its end, `children(kind)`, `stop(kind)`, `label(args)` (the command as typed, 120 characters at most) and `onChange` -- and the `JobStarter` registering each job it starts (`new JobStarter(child, clock, registry)`); `SupervisedJob` gains `finished` and `onEnd`. `JobProgress.ts`, the Mac's `JobProgress`: a job's status, its stages, its bytes, its notices, its documents, its `error` and its `resumeCommand`, folded from its updates alone.
- [x] **The reads** (`business/reads/`): `Models.ts` gains what the place reads -- `ModelRow`'s every field (`roles`, `source`, `format`, `quant`, the MLX kind, the row's `draft`, `name`), `ModelRow.folded`, `MLXKind` (`chats`, `registers`, `reading`), `ModelInfo.read`/`field`/`memory` with the typed facts (`format`, `handle`, `bytes`, `window`, `cache`, `lineage`, `convertsTo`, `kind`) and `RoleAssignment` (the six roles, the suite, the verb) -- and `Adapters.ts`, the Mac's `BackendAdapter` and `AdapterCheck`.
- [x] **The stand-in** (`infrastructure/process/StandInChild.ts`): a rule may be `exact`, answering its arguments whole and no longer line, so `models quantize <h>`'s recording never answers `models quantize <h> -t Q8_0`.
- [x] **The `models` module** (`source/presentation/models/`), the Mac's names:
  - `ViewModels/ModelsViewModel.ts` (980 lines): the tabs, Backends (`models list` under the prototype's two sections; one backend's `models info` as the prototype's FACTS, its DRAFTER, ADAPTERS, LINEAGE and FILES, the CLI's own note on the row), Roles (`models status`'s six, the suite line, each picker its verb, offering what the CLI lets the role name), the pair-drafter and adapters sheets, the `model` and `unregister` confirms, the download sheet's form, and `face()`; it holds and wires the place's other two:
  - `ViewModels/StoreViewModel.ts` (930): Stored weights -- the sets from `models list` and `--all` (the fold), each backend's set by `models info <backend>` then `models info <path>`, each set's `models info`, the selection, the actions by what a set is, the four sheets, the `storeDelete`, `storeModelDelete`, `unregister` and `registerAll` confirms, the narration card, and `land`, what jobs reported they made;
  - `ViewModels/DownloadsViewModel.ts` (684): Jobs -- `DownloadForm` (the prototype's sheet as the CLI's one pull), the pull on the 52b stream, the store's long runs (convert, quantize, check) with their store read first, Stop before the run (GUI 6d), Run again, Resume from … (a store verb the CLI names runs as its store job), Dismiss, the log, each card's face and the done toast; with no binary no job starts and no card is added;
  - `ViewModels/StoreForms.ts` (`ConvertForm`, `QuantizeForm`, `RegisterForm`, `RenameForm`, `StoreStep`, `Estimate`, `storeSize`, `modelBaseName`), `DeletePlan.ts` (`DeletePlan`, `DeleteNarration`) and `BackendAdapters.ts` (`AdaptersView`, `AdaptersForm`);
  - `Views/ModelsScreen.tsx` (`ModelsScreen`, `BackendsTab`, `RolesTab`, `PairDrafterSheet`), `StoreView.tsx` (`StoreView`, `StoreSheet`), `DownloadsView.tsx` (`DownloadsView`, `DownloadSheet`), `AdaptersSheet.tsx`, `DeleteNarrationCard.tsx`;
  - `Previews/`: 37 named previews (`ModelsScreen.preview.tsx` 14, `StoreView.preview.tsx` 12, `DownloadsView.preview.tsx` 7, `AdaptersSheet.preview.tsx` 3, `DeleteNarrationCard.preview.tsx` 1) over `ModelsPreview.ts` (`useModelsPreview`), and `ModelsWorlds.ts`, the place's stand-in answers by world (below).
- [x] **`styles`:** `Components/ModelGlyph.tsx` (`ModelGlyph`, `kindColor`), promoted from the session module on its second use (the Backends table); `session/Views/SessionExtras.tsx` re-exports it, so the session's views keep their imports; `Previews/ModelGlyph.preview.tsx` (2).
- [x] **The composition root:** `AppViewModel` builds the registry and hands it to both job starters (the places' and the console's), builds the place over the stand-in's reads, jobs and clock, wires its doors (the toast, the shell's sheets and confirms, the open session, Settings, Video and Voice), connects it at start, opens the nine presets (`openModelsPreset`), answers its confirms (`confirmModels`, `dismissModels`), lands the inspector's two doors (`openModels(tab, model)`) and gives the sidebar its running counts (`placeRuns`: `models` is every `apogee models pull|convert|quantize|repair` the registry lists). `ApogeeApp` hands the shell `ModelsScreen`, the seven sheets and the five confirms, and `running`. `Presets.ts` adds the nine presets and every preset the Models place's reads (`modelling`, under `listing` and `execute`). `Destination.ts` loses Models' placeholder.

- [x] **Hand-made fixtures** (20; each in the CLI's exact shape, made by a script in the session scratchpad from the recordings named):
