Decisions the item recorded before the build (its document's, kept here since it is deleted in this pass):
- 2026-11-14: **The user's call:** the prototype is rebuilt in React, TypeScript and Vite, with the production apps' four layers and screen modules, so a new app ports from it; it ships in v0.2.1, in the design's own queue.
- 2026-11-14: **The user's call:** the build is one self-contained `main.html` that opens by double-click, the review surface; the canvas becomes view-only and is retired at the end (1t).
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** one module at a time against the frozen old prototype; parity judged on layout, states, controls and wording, not pixels or data; the data the Mac app's recorded fixtures read in place, with hand-made `*-handmade*` files only where nothing was recorded.
- 2026-11-14: **The split:** Compose is its own module, gated on 1k for its shared player and its place in 1k's Video screen.
- (consumed) 1k: the shared player and Video's compositions slot. 1d: where hand-made files live and the preset table's form. 1s: the side-by-side judgment.

The open calls' defaults, taken as stated (2026-11-18, the user's answers before the run):
- **The prototype's four projects are hand-made documents** that keep their titles, briefs and scene counts, so the list reads like the old board; **recorded documents back the new and edit presets**: `compose:new` plans the recorded brief with `compose-planned.jsonl`, and `compose:edit` previews the recorded edit with `compose-edit-preview.json`, its real diff (`from`, `changes`, `removed`) -- not the heat pump (but see the done parent, below).
- **A render is a series of hand-made project documents** (none made, one made, … all made, then `done`) the stand-in serves on its clock, as the files the composer saves after every step (`writes`, above); the screen reads the project beside the video again after each thing the run says, as the Mac reads it. The old task timer is not ported.
- **The stopped project keeps the word "Stopped"** (and "stopped at scene 5 · resumable"); its document says `cancelled`, the CLI's status.
- **The Mac's extra states are not drawn**: a refused run with its install door and the preview's time limit. (The "rendered otherwise" note is drawn: the run's brief reversed this default for it, below.)
- **The "passed over" rows come from a hand-made list the stand-in serves** (`projects-passed-over-handmade.json`, the Mac scan's `Skipped` -- file and reason -- handed in as the place's `passedOver` door and listed after its own scan's), since no CLI command records what a folder scan passed over.

The run's brief (2026-11-18, the user's answers before the run), each for veto:
- **The network was not used.** The npm registry and Playwright's Chromium were allowed and not needed (`package.json` and the lock file unchanged); Google Fonts was not fetched (1c's frames were on disk). No `apogee` binary ran, and nothing composed, rendered or spoke for real: every stream is a recording or hand-made.
- **A Playwright test opening the built file from `file://` stood in for your look**; you try the file afterwards (Not done).
- **Five presets, not four** (the scope override, 2026-11-18): the frozen prototype has `compose:differs` since `28ff6fe8` (board `ScreenComposeDiffers`, the "rendered otherwise" note, `previewedRenders`), and 1s fails on a preset left out, so it is ported here with its note -- reversing the document's default (the note not drawn) for that one state, since the prototype draws it. It needs a hand-made done edit (`compose-render-tides-edit-saved-4-handmade.json`, from `compose-edit-replanned.json`) and the previewed-render comparison (`ProjectLibrary.sameChanges`, the preview kept in memory for its render).
- **`compose:edit` is backed by a hand-made *done* parent** (flagged): `compose-edit-parent.json` is `planned`, every scene's voice and clip null, and the prototype locks the edit pane and hides the player unless the project is rendered, so a faithful port of the recorded parent would draw a locked pane where the board draws an open one with the preview up. `compose-tides-handmade.json` is that recording made: its scenes spoken (`s01.wav`, their seconds and sentences) and rendered (`s01.mp4`), captions kept beside it, `done`; the recorded preview's `from`, `changes` and `removed` fit it unchanged. This departs from the default's wording ("the recorded parent").
- **The CLI's statuses only**: `rendering`, `done`, `failed`, `cancelled`, `planned` -- the old `rendered` is `done`, its `stopped` `cancelled`; a fixture-shape test holds every hand-made file to them.
- **The chosen voice is read through a service the app root passes in** (flagged): `MediaPreferences` in `styles` (beside the theme service, the other window-wide preference service), built once by `AppViewModel` from `settingsPreferences.voice` and handed to Settings (which shows and changes it), to Compose (whose fresh new composition starts from it) and to Voice (1m, whose "Use this voice for compositions" changes it) -- 1i's hand-off ("a shared service … is 1m's and 1l's to promote"). Compose never imports `settings`.
  - 2026-11-21 (the review's fixes, flagged): **the service holds captions too.** Settings' Caption new compositions had stayed in `SettingsPreferences`, so unticking it reached no new composition; the old `freshCompose` starts the draft from `mediaCaptions` (`main.html` 4674). `MediaPreferences(voice, captions)` now holds it (`captions`, `toggleCaptions`), built from `settingsPreferences.captions`, and a fresh draft starts from it -- an open one keeps its own box, as the old `nc ?? freshCompose()` does.
  - 2026-11-21 (the review's fixes, flagged): **an open new composition keeps its voice when Settings changes it**, correcting "follows it while open" above, which was true of Voice's button alone: the old Settings sets `defaultVoice` only (`main.html` 6256), and only Voice's "Use this voice for compositions" sets `nc.voice` too (7692). So `chooseVoice` (Settings) reaches a fresh draft, and `useForCompositions` (1m's button) the default and an open draft, through `onUseForCompositions`.
- **The project documents come from the folder 1k's clips come from** (`~/Movies/Apogee` in the preset's `folders`, scanned by `ProjectLibrary.projects` as the Mac scans it); **presets live in `compose/Previews/`**, composed in `app/Previews/Presets.ts`.
- **A render is a job on 1h's shared list**, so the sidebar's Video badge counts it with 1k's clips; compose draws no install and raises no consent.
- **The path is `source/presentation/compose/`**; 1q:27's `src/` is not this item's.

Decisions taken while building (2026-11-21, an unattended group run), each for veto:
- **The module's edges** (flagged): `compose` declares `styles` as its only Presentation edge -- "Compose declares `styles` alone" -- and the lower layers its view-model and previews use (`clock`, `process`, `protocol`, `jobs`, `media`, `reads`), as the Mac's `ComposeViewModel` imports `BusinessJobs`, `BusinessMedia`, `BusinessReads`, `DataProtocol` and `InfrastructureProcess`. No `shell`: its doors are closures the app root fills.
- **A new composition starts from the recorded brief** (`the tides, twice a day`, handed in as `startingBrief`) where the old form started from its own sourdough brief, which nothing recorded: so Plan is answered live by `compose-planned.jsonl`. **Save as is the Mac's free name for the brief** (`the-tides-twice-a-day.mp4`, `MediaLibrary.stem`'s five words, as the Mac's own test plans it) and is pinned when Plan runs, so Render renders the plan kept beside it -- **a defect fixed**: the old renamed the output from the plan's title once planned (`sourdough-rises.mp4`), so its Render would have written elsewhere than its plan was kept. The recorded stream's document still names `tides.mp4`, the recording's own, as the Mac's test replays it for another output.
- **The plan is held in the draft**, as the old `nc.plan` holds it: the stand-in's dry run writes nothing into the folder (the CLI keeps the planned project beside the video, and the Mac's list would show it); the draft's row says "planned · nothing rendered". Render's stream says "rendering the plan a dry run kept at …", the CLI's words.
- **An edit's output is the Mac's** (`<name>-edit.mp4`, free): `tides-edit.mp4` is the recording's own; the old drew `heat-pump-2.mp4`.
- **The command line keeps the old words; the run is the Mac's order** (as 1k's): the drawn command puts the brief first and `-o` last, `--voice` only for a voice other than the CLI's `af_heart`, `-m` only for a pipeline other than the default, `--planner` only for another than the chat role's, `--voice-model` where the store holds two or more; the job runs `ComposeForm`'s list (`-o OUT … -- brief`), the same flags. The Mac's form passes `--voice` whenever set; the place sets it only where the old command names it.
- **`--planner` and an edit's settings** (flagged): the TypeScript `ComposeForm` gains the CLI's `--planner` (the old Planner select; the Mac draws none) and, on an edit, `--voice`, `-m`, `--music` and `--no-captions` where the new video changes them (the old edit command; the Mac's edit passes none). Captions turned on for a project without them passes nothing: no flag turns them on, and the note says so.
- **The planners are the world's backends** (flagged): the old `gpt-oss-20b` (local) and Claude Sonnet (API) are `root` (a local llamacpp backend) and `claude` (the anthropic backend) in the hand-made projects' `plan.backend`; a new composition's planner is the chat role (`fastroot`, a mock backend, whose kind `ModelKind` never guesses, so its notes say no kind). The recorded projects' `planner` backend is said as it is.
- **The pipelines and voices offered are the store's**: the video world's `models list` (Settings' sets with Wan 2.2's pipeline beside its MLX conversion) and each voice model's `models info` voices (`af_heart · default`, `am_adam`, `bf_emma`), a project's own value listed first where the store lacks it (`org--clip/pipeline/aaaaaaaaaaaa`, `af_bella`, `bm_george`), where the old listed 28 invented voices. The hand-made projects ran on the default MLX pipeline (`Wan2.2-TI2V-5B/mlx/5e6f708192a3`) with the PyTorch Kokoro set. **The voice-model picker over `compose-voices-list.json` is a preview** (`VoiceModels`): as every preset's `models list` that recording would leave every set's `models info` unanswered (the Models place reads them on every preset, 1h's rule), so the presets read the world's list, which names two Kokoro sets too.
- **The captions line and remedy are `check`'s `ffmpeg captions` row** (`CaptionsAbility`, the recorded `check-captions-skipped.json` on every preset), in the Mac's words with the old's "To burn them in: " before the remedy; the plan card's CAPTIONS line says the old words where the row is skipped, "burned into the frames" where it is ok.
- **The hand-made streams carry five `step` events a clip** (10, 20, 30, 40, 50 of 50) where the CLI relays every step (flagged): one line a tick, a clip then renders in about ten ticks, near the old six, where fifty would take 20 s a clip. Every other event is the 52b protocol's: `scene`, `ready` (the voice on the cpu, the clip on mps), `segment`, `frames`, `assembling`, the notices in `compose.cpp`'s and `video_compose.cpp`'s words, and the project last.
- **A render's documents are saved after each step's last line** (the voice's segment, the clip's assembling, the document) -- the first, nothing made, as the run starts (the review's fixes, below) -- each with a `cancelled` twin a Stop leaves (`error: "cancelled"`, the CLI's `stop`), its scenes' files as the composer names them (`<stem>.scenes/s01.wav`, `s01.mp4`, an asset's own extension), clip seconds the CLI's `scene_length` (the plan's, or the narration's measured length and its room), sentences timed by their share of the narration as the old cues are, and `assembly` in `ffmpeg.cpp`'s words. The renders' documents are `created` at the stand-in's now; the old four keep the old times (Today 09:40; Yesterday 21:30, 20:48, 20:05).
- **The list is newest first** (the Mac scan's order): the tide (Yesterday 20:48) lists before the night market (20:05), where the old's hand-written order put it last.
- **The stop note says what the document records** (flagged): a project read stopped says "Stopped at scene 5’s clip. Every finished scene is kept …" -- its first unmade scene -- without the old's "step 23 of 50, when Apogee quit at 21:12 yesterday", which no project document records; a Stop pressed here says the step its run had reached (`Stopped at scene 6’s clip, step 20 of 50. …`), as the old does.
- **The phases are the document's and the run's**: spoken and made counted from the project's files, the step and the scene from the stream's `scene` and `step` events; Assemble runs once every scene is made; its detail the project's crossfade, bed and captions. A failed project says "failed at scene N · resumable" and its error (no recording makes one).
- **One render at a time, refused in the old words**: Resume while one renders says "One composition renders at a time. Stop the other first."; an edit's Render, and the new composition's Render, say "… Wait for the other, or stop it." -- **a defect fixed**: the old new-composition Render started a second render over the first. A plan or an edit's preview on paper runs beside a render, as the old allows; neither is a run the badge or the monitor counts.
- **Stop and Resume through the stand-in** (flagged): a Stop leaves the `cancelled` twin of the last project saved, and the same command resumes from it -- its first line, then the next step -- so Resume renders only what the project lacks after any stop (`StandInChild.stopped`'s memory, beside `until`'s). The tide's stored stop is its own recording's start (`compose-resume-tide-pools-handmade.jsonl`, "resuming …: 7 of 7 narrations and 4 clips already there").
- **An edit's preview** is `--dry-run --output-format json`, the edited project read whole from its lines, held to the old 4 ticks (`exitAfter`); its diff in the CLI's own words (`compose.cpp`'s `diff_lines`: "the edit: 2 scenes, were 2", "scene 1, untouched", "scene 2; its prompt changed", "a new seed", "its length changed"), which are the old's; any change to the form -- the words, a re-roll, a setting -- drops it (Save as alone does not); a preview that lands for a form changed meanwhile is dropped unsaid. Render plans again with the preview's `plan.backend` ("planner", the recording's); before a preview, the pass is named by the chat role (the review's fixes, below). A preview no recording answers ends in the stand-in's words as the pane's red line (`PreviewFailed`).
- **The new composition's sample bed and folder are the old's** (`~/Music/beds/warm-kitchen.m4a`, `~/Pictures/bakery/` with its "2 files: crumb-close.jpg, oven-door.mov"), since a browser picks no file -- 1k's rule for its samples; the edit pane's beds are the project's own and `soft-piano.m4a`, under `~/Music/beds/`.
- **Every preset carries the old four**, the Video presets too, so Compositions counts 4 on every screen as the old tab does (1k's tab drew no count); the recorded Tides is only in `compose:edit`'s and `compose:differs`' folders, so `compose` and its siblings list the old four.
- **The stub's view, view-model, preview and test were written anew at their paths**; the stub preview `Stub` is gone, and nothing else was deleted.
- **The document's own decisions were recorded here, not in it:** it is deleted in the same pass.

Fixed after review (2026-11-21, the same unattended run: reviewers confirmed these against the built tree and the frozen prototype; each fixed test-first, the decisions flagged for veto):
- **Caption new compositions reached no new composition** (a defect fixed): the shared `MediaPreferences` holds captions beside the voice, and a fresh draft starts from them (the decision's dated lines above).
- **An open new composition followed a voice changed in Settings** (a defect fixed): only "Use this voice for compositions" reaches it, `useForCompositions` (above); 1m's document and DEVELOPER.md name it.
- **A re-roll could not be previewed** though the CLI's answers are recorded: `compose-edit-redo.json` (scene 2 alone, with its `.err`) and `compose-edit-reroll-preview.json` (scene 1 beside the recorded instruction) are wired as live answers (`redoPreviewArguments`, `rerollPreviewArguments`); `compose:edit` stays on `compose-edit-preview.json`. Neither re-roll renders: nothing recorded a render of them.
- **The monitor's render row offered Stop the run** where the old `crender` row offers Open: it now opens (`MonitorRun.opens`, `MonitorViewModel.openRun`): the monitor closed onto Video › Compositions at the render; Stop and Resume stay on the project page.
- **Render showed an empty tab until the stream's second line, and a Stop then lost the composition** (a defect fixed): a stand-in write `after: 0` lands as the job starts (`SupervisedJob.replay`, never on a resume, whose own write follows its first line), and the new render's and the edit's first saves moved there (`saves([0, …])`), so the project is in the list, rendering, from Render on, as the old adds it, and a Stop at once leaves its `cancelled` twin, resumable. Flagged: the edit's first save now precedes the stream's "editing … with planner" line, which in the CLI comes before its planning call; the old's row appears at once too.
- **A plan that landed after its brief or assets changed was shown as the new brief's** (a defect fixed): `plan()` takes the form it planned and drops the plan when the words changed meanwhile, as the edit pane drops a preview. Flagged: it says "The brief changed while it was planned: plan it again", words the old never needed (it planned the words in the box when its timer ended, which a recording cannot).
- **The edit pass named the project's planner** (flagged): the CLI's `video compose --edit` plans with the chat role unless `--planner` names one (`video_compose.cpp`, `resolve_chat_backend`), and the pane passes none, so Make changes says "One pass by fastroot" (billed only where the chat role is an API backend) until a preview names its own planner, one value for the note, the Render line and its help -- where the old said the project's (`planM`, `main.html` 7468).
- **An asset taken as it is was told by an empty visual**, which the CLI never writes (its reader refuses "a scene has no narration or no visual") (flagged): the port uses the CLI's rule, by the asset's extension (`isStill`, `ProjectRecord.takesAsItIs`, as `compose.cpp`'s `is_still`); the night market's scene 3 gained a visual describing its clip, and the screen still says "takes stall-0412.mov as it is". The fixture-shape test refuses a scene with no narration or no visual. A still is no longer counted made before its clip renders.
- **The Tides edit's render left out the clip the CLI carries for scene 1** (a defect fixed): its narration alone changed, so `carry_scenes` carries the parent's `s01.mp4` (3.00 s), then the clip pass finds it shorter than the 3.41 s its new narration needs and renders it again, its seed held. Saves 1 and 2 and their stopped twins carry the clip, the stream says so in `compose.cpp`'s words (20 lines, `3.41 s`), its saves after 0, 6, 17 and 20 lines; and a clip counts made only once the composer keeps it (`ProjectRecord.keepsClip`: spoken and `clip + 0.1 ≥ 0.25 + narration + 0.25 + crossfade`), in the phases, the timeline, the scenes and the stop note -- so Assemble never runs early -- and a clip about to render again does not set its scene's length (3.41 s while it renders, 3.4 s as measured).
- **The Rendering preview stood at scene 2's last step**, not at scene 1's clip as it says: a still frame replays its 12 start ticks into the render too, so it plays 2 more, not 14 (checked in the clone: "rendering scene 1 · step 20 of 50").
- **Three guards had no teeth:** the tide's second Resume now checks it goes on from scene 6 ("rendering scene 6 · step 1 of 50", "5 of 7 made"); a form changed while its preview plans drops that preview (the words, a re-roll, a setting); `sameChanges` compares the parent's scenes left out (the Mac's `ProjectsTests.removal`).

Every region, control and state is drawn: the header with both tabs and their counts, the pipeline line and New composition; the list with its draft row, rows, EDIT tags, statuses, the two passed-over lines and its foot; the new composition's heading, brief and count, Plan or its planning line, the plan card with its scenes, PLAN, SPEND, CAPTIONS and MUSIC, Render and Plan again; its settings (Planner and its note, Pipeline, Voice, Voice model, Music bed, Assets, Captions with its line and remedy, Save as, the limits, the command); a project's title, tag, meta, status, Stop or Resume, brief, the three phases, the stop note, CHANGES with the "rendered otherwise" warning, the player with its caption, the timeline's four tracks and legend, SCENES with Re-roll, FILES, SETTINGS and CHAIN; and Make changes (the locked line, the instruction and its note, the re-roll line, the four settings, Preview the changes or its planning line and Stop, the preview card with its diff, notes, spend and copy line, the replan line, Save as, Render as a new video, Discard, the command). a store with one voice model; scene 1 3.4 s as its clip measured), and a rerun killed all four: 37 of 37. **The review's pass** (2026-11-21, a fresh `git clone --shared` of `63ff2d2b` under the session scratchpad, sparse as before, the working tree laid over it, `node_modules` linked, nothing set in `GIT_*`) ran 24 mutants, each against the tests it should trip -- a resume never passed to the job, a resume skipping nothing, a preview kept when its form changed mid-flight, the removed scenes not compared, a draft not captioned from the preference, Settings keeping its own captions, Settings' voice reaching an open draft, Use this voice for compositions missing it, either re-roll preview unwired, the render row stopping, Open stopping, Open selecting nothing, no write as the job starts, the start write laid over a resume, a stale plan kept, an assets change ignored, a still taken as it is, the extension case-sensitive, the fixture visual check off, the project's planner naming the edit pass, a clip made by its file alone, the timing keeping a short clip, no tenth spared -- 24 of 24 killed.

### 2026-11-21 — `voice-screen` (backlog item 1m): the Voice place in the React app

Decisions the item recorded before the build (its document's, kept here since it is deleted in this pass):
- 2026-11-14: **The user's call:** the prototype is rebuilt in React, TypeScript and Vite with the production GUIs' four layers and MVVM modules, and stays the design's cross-platform source of truth.
- 2026-11-14: **The user's call:** the build is one self-contained `lib/design/main.html` that opens with a double-click; the design has its own queue, and this is v0.2.1.
- 2026-11-14: **The user's call:** the canvas becomes view-only and is retired at the end (1t); review happens in the built file; parity screenshots are Playwright's, in 1s.
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** data is the Mac app's recorded fixtures, read in place at build time; a state nobody recorded gets a `*-handmade*` file in the CLI's shape; view-models are plain TypeScript classes that never import React.
- 2026-11-14: **The recorded default:** each module is ported against the frozen old prototype; parity is judged on layout, states, controls and wording, not pixels or data.
- 2026-11-14: **The split:** Library and Review are one item, two modules sharing the sheet host, 10 presets.
- (consumed) 1d: the preset type, the clock, the `apogee` modes, where hand-made files live. 1e: the sheet host, the confirm host, the shell's places and keys. 1q: the gallery rows for the nine boards and whether `library:unsaved` gets one. 1s: the side-by-side parity screenshots.

The Mac's create form, delete, enable and register, run, post, sweep and removal are new design.

Decisions taken while building (2026-11-21, an unattended group run), each for veto:
- **The agents' page is chosen by name** (flagged): `agents-list.json` lists `security-review` first and `triage` eighth of eight; the old page shows triage only because its canned list put it first. The CLI's order is kept and `shownAgent` names `triage`, so the agents tab shows its page as the old does.
- **The editor's files are the stand-in's folders** (flagged): the Mac reads the data directory from `config path` and the agent's prompt from `<data>/prompts/triage.txt` with Foundation, and a symphony's spec from the path `symphonies list` names; the port reads both through the preset's `folders` (`~/.apogee/prompts`, `~/.apogee/symphonies`, on every preset), and a save writes there through `FolderWriting`. So `config path` is a hand-made answer (no recording has it), and with no apogee or a config the stand-in refuses, no data directory is read and no editor opens.
- **The triage prompt ends with the binary's newline** (flagged): the Swift literal drops it (a multi-line literal's last newline), `starter_prompt` writes it, and the old frame's blank line before "File each issue under one area." needs it.
- **The save takes the old prototype's 4 ticks on the clock** (flagged): the stand-in answers a read at once, so the editor's owner writes the file, holds the editor read-only, waits `saveTicks` (4) on the injected clock, then reads back (`agents list`, or `symphonies show <name>`) and lands the verdict on the editor as it stands -- the Mac's order. A still frame never ticks, so a save made in one stands saving, as the old frame would.
- **`library:refused` is reached through the CLI's own answer**, never set by hand: the preset types the refused spec and saves; `symphonies show notes-digest` judges the file by what it holds, as the old prototype's save judges the text (`main.html:3011-3019`): while the stand-in's `notes-digest.yaml` holds `temprature:` it answers `symphony-show-unknown-key.err` (exit 1) -- the verdict the CLI's words with "(exited with status 1)", word for word the old's -- and otherwise `symphony-show.json`, so Put it back as it was is read again and stands, closing the editor. It is one rule on every preset (the stand-in's `refusedWhile`, a rule refused while a folder file holds a text), so the same text saved again is refused again and Close asks again, on any preset. (Amended 2026-11-21 after review: the first build answered the preset's first `show` with the refusal and every later one with the document, so a second save of the refused text read as saved and Close then closed without asking; any other preset read a refused text as saved.)
- **The schema's Edit keeps the old toast** (flagged): the folders hold no `schemas/triage-output.json` (nothing recorded it), so Edit says "Edited in the app, saved to schemas/triage-output.json (prototype)", word for word, where the Mac would open the file.
- **Play runs `symphonies play` as a supervised job** (the Mac's `playIt`, flagged): critique-revise's recorded play answers its exact arguments (`--input "A draft README for the tool."`, the Mac test's input and the recording's), stdout `symphony-play.json`, its stage lines `symphony-play.err` played as the busy line (1j's `busy`); while it runs the page draws the CLI's stage line with the old sheet's busy pulse (a running state the old never drew, made from its own busy line), then each stage's answer and the output on its own page only. A play nobody recorded -- any other symphony -- ends in the stand-in's words in the toast, where the old toasted its command with "(prototype)". Picking another symphony clears the last play with the image, as the old pick does (`played: r[0] === lbSym ? lbs.played : false`, `main.html:6611`), where the Mac keeps it on its own page; re-picking the symphony shown keeps it, and a running play goes on and lands on its own page (amended 2026-11-21 after review: the first build kept the play, so critique-revise came back with its answers and OUTPUT).
- **The page's inputs and image are the old prototype's words**, handed in by the composition (`useSamples`): critique-revise's, describe-answer's and notes-digest's inputs from the old `lbSyms`, others empty (Play off until something is typed, the Mac's `canPlay`); Choose an image… offers the old sample, `/Users/someone/Pictures/storefront.png`, since a browser picks no file.
- **A server's removal runs `config delete-mcp-server code`** (flagged): answered by a hand-made line (`mcp-delete.txt` recorded it for `notes`), read back by `mcp list`, the CLI's line in the toast; the stand-in keeps no state, so the removal the CLI reported is kept beside the read and the page empties, as the old `mcpGone` does.
- **`mcp presets` is the preset's** (flagged): every preset answers it with Settings' world (`xcode-mcp-presets.json`, three presets, 1i); the Library presets answer it with `mcp-presets.json`, the old's two, their own rule winning. Library reached from another preset lists `xcode` too.
- **Review's dry run is the presets'** (flagged): `review` and `review:post` run the Mac's `review()` in their setup -- the old form's merge request, `--dry-run`, answered for its exact arguments by `review-run-dry.json` -- and the frame's 2 ticks land it; `review:post` asks to post once it has landed. The tab's Review button stays dead (the default), so on any other preset the Reviews tab draws the form and its command and no run; with no apogee nothing runs, and in `config-typo` the run's refusal is said in the toast.

No separate post view: the words are the view-model's (`postWords`, the Mac's `postWords`).

The MCP command is the CLI's command and arguments in full (the Mac's), where the old shortened it to `python3 ~/.apogee/…`.
- **Two Edit buttons and each Remove… name what they act on** for a screen reader (`Edit prompts/triage.txt`, `Remove widget…`), where the old buttons were all called Edit or Remove….
- **The module's edges** (flagged): `library` and `review` on `clock`, `process`, `protocol`, `jobs` and `reads` besides `shell` and `styles` (the Mac's `PresentationLibrary` and `PresentationReview` import their Business twins; the previews build the stand-in).
- **The stand-in's `config-typo` mode passes `review skills` to the rules** (`StandInChild.configFree`; 2026-11-21, after review, flagged): the CLI's `review skills list` and `show` read only the skills folder (`review_cmd.cpp:74-75,106-107`), the Mac's `ReviewPreview.badConfig` lists them over a bad config, and the old prototype draws the seven lenses there, so Review's Skills tab and the add-target sheet's lenses read as at `installed`. The first build refused every call, and the tab said a refusal the CLI never prints. Decisions taken while building (2026-11-21, an unattended group run), each for veto:
- **The Knowledge tab is the composition's** (`AppViewModel.knowledgeTab`, flagged): the document says the tab is the route's, so no third view-model holds it; the Mac's `KnowledgeScreen` keeps it as `@State tab`, and the screen takes it and `pickTab` from its owner, which keeps it across visits as the old `kn.tab` stays.
- **One hand-made `embed list` of the old three** (the brief's second option, flagged): `collections-list.json` lists knowledge and notes, `collections-list-vectors.json` mixed and notes, from two captures; the merged list takes knowledge's and notes' rows from the first and mixed's from the second, so every preset lists the old three with notes at 5 · 5, as the old does. The second capture's notes (3 sources, 3 chunks, `collection-info-vectors.json`, one model) is not drawn: a data difference against that recording.
- **The knowledge store's own collection has a hand-made page** (flagged), where the old fell back to notes' page ("has no recorded page, so it shows notes' as before").
- **With no collection chosen, notes' page is shown and its row current**, as the old's `kn.coll` starts at notes (amended 2026-11-21 after review: the first build drew no row current until one was chosen).
- **A branch picked clears a standing question**, as the old `onBranch` sets `asked: false`; the Mac's 6h asks it again under the branch (`knowledge query --status rejected`, unrecorded).
- **The raw conversation is offered wherever the record has an archive** (`raw_ref`, the Mac's rule), where the old offered it on the rejected record alone; all three carry one, so the other two say the stand-in's words in place when shown.
- **A question, a search or a raw read nobody recorded is said in place**, in the error color -- under the field, under the search, in the raw section -- where the Mac raises an alert and the old has no failure; "No superseded records." (the Mac's words) stands in an empty branch, where the old drew nothing.
- **The eleventh toast is the composition's list** (`recordedSearches`, `notes` alone): a search of a collection whose search the CLI never recorded says `apogee embed query <name>: its lines, as printed (prototype)` and runs nothing.
- **CAPTURED is the CLI's timestamp whole** (`2026-10-21T04:59:44.463192Z`), where the old cut it to seconds.
- **A node's card is the picture's**, as the old one is (flagged): kind the CLI's type (FUNCTION, CLASS, FILE, where the old said METHOD), the name past the module its file names (`Runner.run_batch`, `GraphNode.label`), where (`ingest/runner.py:8`, a file's `ingest/runner.py · 17 lines` from its `end_line`), the CLI's description as the signature (`def run_batch(self, batch)`), CALLED BY and CALLS from the picture's `calls` edges, IMPORTED BY and IMPORTS from its `imports`; no docstring paragraph (the CLI records none). The canvas cards' second line is the place, or a file's lines.
- **`graph explain` is asked only in a picture an update elsewhere overtook** (flagged): the old's `gStale` is a state its preset sets, and the stand-in keeps no history of a graph changed elsewhere, so `graph:refused`'s setup calls `overtaken()`; in that picture a click is explained first (the Mac's `select`), `Runner._fetch` refused by `graph-explain-gone-fetch-handmade.err` (the alert over the kept canvas, OK alone, its OK the Mac's Clear), and every other drawn node, in Functions and in Files, answered by `explain.json` -- the one explain recorded of `demo`, `Runner`'s; the code views never draw it, the card being the picture's -- so it shows its card, as the old `pickNode` refuses `_fetch` alone (amended 2026-11-21 after review: the first build answered `Runner` alone, so any other click raised the stand-in's words in the alert); Rebuild reads the graph again and ends it; and only a node the picture draws is explained (with the graph unread, as at `config-typo`, nothing is asked). `graph-explain-gone.err` is the refusal's shape, not its text: it names `ingest.runner.helper`, a node of another state of `demo`, so a click on `_fetch` answered with it would name a node not clicked -- the old `graphRefusal()` made its own in that recording's shape the same way.
- **`GraphLayout` departs from the Mac's algorithm after it** (flagged; the document's guardrail at :83 against :42 and :65): the Mac's seeded Fruchterman-Reingold layout is ported bit for bit (SplitMix64 in BigInt, its first draws pinned against values computed apart in Python), then scaled onto the old 820 × 520 canvas and given a separation pass -- each overlapping pair pushed apart along the axis it overlaps least, kept inside the canvas, and where 400 passes cannot clear them each card moved to the nearest free cell of a 5 × 10 grid of cards in the layout's reading order -- because the Mac draws dots and the old draws 160 × 44 cards, 40 of which cover about 66 % of the canvas. A planted 40-node document places with no overlap inside the canvas through the grid; every size from 1 to 40 places clean.
- **A view is laid out over every relation between its nodes and draws its own** (flagged): Functions draws the code entities and their `calls`, Files the files and their `imports`; laid out over its calls alone, the demo picture's six cards fell into two corners, `run_batch` outside the frame.
- **An edge joins the facing sides** (flagged): right middle to left middle, as the old draws its left-to-right picture, and left to right or top to bottom where the layout puts the target elsewhere, so no edge runs back across its own cards.
- **The canvas brings the selected card into view** (flagged): it scrolls past its frame as the old one does; at 1440 × 900 about 570 px of its 820 show, so the view scrolls the least that shows the selected card.
- **The header says no build time** (flagged): "~/src · 4 files · 16 symbols" from `stats.json` (its source, files and nodes), where the old said "· built 14:02"; `graph stats` reports no time, and the design is honest by default. A gone graph's header is "0 nodes · 0 edges · 0 files", the Mac's over a deleted graph's stats and the old's words.
- **A delete the CLI reported is kept beside the reads** until a rebuild, since the stand-in keeps no state (`graph stats demo` still answers built).
- **Rebuild runs `graph update demo`** (the Mac's, the graph having sources), answered by a one-line hand-made stream (flagged): `graph.cpp`'s own last line, `  Code graph:       16 node(s), 21 edge(s) (nodes +16 ~0 -0; edges +21 -0)` -- `stats.json`'s counts, and the sync suffix of the delete-then-Rebuild path, every node and edge added (an untouched graph's Rebuild would print `+0 ~0 -0`, and the stateless stand-in answers both the same, so the one answer is the path that changes what is drawn, the graph brought back; amended 2026-11-21 after review, the first build having played the Mac test's literal without the suffix). Left out are the build's earlier lines (`Updating the code of …`, Files used, References), whose References counts no recording holds; the old's "Graph rebuilt · 214 files · 1,284 symbols" was invented. Notes' Rebuild (`graph build notes`, a model's run) is unrecorded and says the stand-in's words (below).
- **The save panel saves only under the CLI's own name** (flagged): each export and the report answer for their exact arguments (`/Users/someone/Documents/demo.html` and its siblings), said as recorded; another name ends in the stand-in's words. The guardrail's "with the chosen file name" is read as the name chosen in the command; the old rewrote the typed name into its canned line, which no CLI prints. The notes graph's exports are unrecorded.
- **The dedupe's dry run is a command, its merge a job** (the Mac's): the dry run's text answers at once (the old's two-tick "reading" busy line never shows; the stand-in answers a command at once); the merge plays `graph-dedupe-merged-handmade.txt`'s three lines a tick each, the sheet saying "merging" with Done off, then the merged text.
- **The Files view's question** is the four files' paths, the hand-made picture's own; the Functions view's is `query.json`'s, "runner fetch" -- neither drawn, the old having no search.
- **Ask about this goes through the open session's leave** (flagged): `SessionLeave`'s `new` gained `attach` and `draft`, so the library opens a new session on Sessions, attaches `/Users/someone/src/ingest/runner.py` (the graph's source and the node's file, where the old's chip said `src/ingest/runner.py`) and writes "Explain Runner.run_batch and what depends on it."; a private conversation is asked about first (the old did not ask, its private chat surviving beside the new one). It is a callback the app root fills; `graph` imports no `session`.
- **The prose pane explains the busiest entity** (the report's first hub, Heat pump), where the old fixed Heat pump: the same frame.
- **The graph's refresh reads the view it was asked for**, so a route change's read and a preset's view change cannot cross (found while wiring `graph:prose`: the read begun on the Functions view asked `graph report --graph demo`).
- **The module edges** (above) and the confirms: `explainRefused` OK alone; `graphDelete` Cancel and the destructive Delete the graph.
- **An empty field asks the old's recorded question** (flagged; 2026-11-21, after review): Ask with nothing typed asks "why was the cancel button removed" and fills the field, as the old `kno.ask` always shows it asked, and Search with nothing typed on notes asks "how does a heat pump work", as `kno.search` does -- where the Mac asks nothing. The questions are handed in by the composition (`RecordsViewModel.emptyQuestion`, `CollectionsViewModel.emptyQuestions`), as `recordedSearches` is, so neither view-model imports the preview world. Under a branch the Mac's query is `--status <branch>`, which no recording holds, so an Ask there says the stand-in's words in place (the old showed the three scored whatever the branch); with every branch it answers as recorded.
- **Notes' exports, its report and its Rebuild stay unrecorded** (flagged; 2026-11-21, after review, which found them ending in the stand-in's words where the old toasted canned lines): `graph export <format> --graph notes`, `graph report --graph notes --out …` and `graph build notes` have no recording, and a hand-made answer would have to invent what the CLI computes -- each export's and the report's size in KB, the mermaid picture of a prose graph, and a model's extraction run -- so they say the stand-in's words, and once notes' graph is deleted it comes back only on a reload. The capture that would align the graph's recordings (Not done) would add them.
- **The document's own decisions were recorded here, not in it:** it is deleted in the same pass. The stubs were written anew at their paths; nothing was deleted but this item's document.

**Side by side with the frozen old prototype** (1c's 6 frames, each kept at `installed` alone -- `screens/knowledge@installed.png`, `knowledge_collections`, `knowledge_mixed`, `graph`, `graph_prose`, `graph_refused` -- against Playwright shots of the built file at `?start=<preset>&still=true&apogee=installed`, 1440 × 900). Every region, tab, control and state of each frame is drawn. The differences, each intended or a defect fixed:
- **`knowledge`, `knowledge:collections`, `knowledge:mixed`, `graph:prose`:** word for word but CAPTURED, the CLI's timestamp whole (above).
- **`graph`:** the CLI's data -- the header's `~/src · 4 files · 16 symbols` with no build time; the tree the demo's four files under `ingest/` (the old's `src/ingest/` with `config.py`, `retry.py`, `parsers/feed.py` and `tests/`); six cards where the old drew nine, placed by the Mac's layout with the separation pass, the canvas scrolled to the selected card; the card's FUNCTION, `ingest/runner.py:8`, the CLI's signature and no docstring; CALLED BY · 0 and CALLS · 1 (`Runner._fetch`) where the old called it from `main` and had it call `parse_feed` too.
- **`graph:refused`:** the alert's words name the demo graph and its nodes; the canvas is `graph`'s, `Runner._fetch` highlighted with no pane, as the old.
- **Defects found in this build and fixed before it shipped:** the selected card outside the canvas's frame, and edges drawn back across their own cards (each above).
- **Not 1o's and in every preset's frame:** the sidebar's chats (1g's: the `Review PR 482` row's glyph), the SYSTEM strip's missing memory line (1e's), the Tasks icon's missing dot (1p's).
- **No other defect was found in the frames.** The review's, behind them, are under Verified. This record is handed to 1s's document (parity-proof.md's Decisions made).

**Verified.**
- **Test first.** Watched failing before their code: `Knowledge.test.ts` and `Graph.test.ts` (their modules missing), `GraphLayout.test.ts` (its module missing; then the facing-sides edge case on its first rule), `RecordsViewModel.test.ts` and `CollectionsViewModel.test.ts` (their modules missing), `GraphViewModel.test.ts` (its module missing; then the delete's read-back and the communities toast's graph, each corrected in the code or the expectation; then the drawn-node guard), the 8 composition tests in `AppViewModel.test.ts` (8 of 8, before the presets and wiring), the new `SessionLibraryViewModel.test.ts` case (the leave with a file and a draft), and `KeyMap.test.ts`' placeholder line. **Written with or after their code** (said plainly): the per-preset walk's additions and its two planted tests, the fixture check (`GraphFixtures.test.ts`, its planted names proving it), the planted layering test (passing at once: it pins 1b's rules on the two modules), and the 23 Playwright tests, written after the first 1o build and run against it: 5 failed, four on the tests' own locators and one on the build -- `graph:refused` at `config-typo` raising the alert over a graph it could not read, which the drawn-node guard fixed, its view-model test watched failing first.
- **The guards have teeth.** A mutation pass in a `git clone --shared` of this checkout under the session scratchpad (this working tree's changes copied over it, `node_modules` linked, no `GIT_*` set by the run) ran 10 mutants: no separation pass, no grid fallback, every dry run offering Merge, Files drawing calls, a branch keeping the question, asking keeping the list's order, every collection searched, Ask about this dropping the file, every click explained, a delete leaving the graph drawn -- 10 of 10 killed.
- **The review's fixes (2026-11-21), each test first.** Reviewers confirmed seven defects (ten reports, three of them twice), each fixed in this working tree: at `missing` both places drew the Mac's "Apogee isn't installed" line, which the document's default says neither draws (now no words, above); the rebuild's hand-made line was the Mac test's literal, not the CLI's shape (now `graph.cpp`'s line with its sync suffix, above); on `graph:refused`, after OK, every click but `Runner` raised the stand-in's words in the alert where the old shows the card (now every drawn node but `_fetch` is answered, above); notes' row was never current with no collection chosen, though the old's is (above); Ask and Search did nothing with the field empty, where the old's always show the recorded state (above); 1s's document said this side-by-side found no defect (it names the two above now); and the list of off-size recordings left out `graph-dedupe-dry-run.txt` (added above, in `DEVELOPER.md` and in `GraphWorldRules.ts`' header) -- and the records' stale-reply guard had no test (two cases added). Watched failing before their code: the four missing-mode expectations (`RecordsViewModel`, `CollectionsViewModel`, `GraphViewModel` and `AppViewModel` tests); the rebuild line's two (`GraphViewModel.test.ts`' toast, and `GraphFixtures.test.ts` now holding the file to `graph.cpp`'s format over `stats.json`'s counts); the overtaken picture's two (a new `GraphViewModel.test.ts` case -- after OK, `run_batch`, `main`, a Files card and a tree pick each show their card, the alert never raised again and nothing unanswered -- and `AppViewModel.test.ts`' `graph:refused` case extended); notes' current row (`CollectionsViewModel.test.ts`); the empty fields' three (`RecordsViewModel.test.ts`' Ask case rewritten, with a branch and a blank field; a new `CollectionsViewModel.test.ts` case; the ⌘8 composition case extended); and 5 Playwright tests run against the build before the fixes (the three presets' `missing` frames, the live Knowledge test's empty Ask and Search and notes' current row, and the live `graph:refused` test's clicks on `Runner.run_batch` and `cli.py`). The two stale-reply cases passed at once, the guard being in place, and were watched failing in a sparse `git clone --shared` of this checkout under the session scratchpad (the prototype, `tokens.json` and the Mac's test fixtures; this working tree's prototype copied over it, `node_modules` linked; no `GIT_*` set by the run, the shell's own `GIT_EDITOR` inherited) with the guard's line removed; two more mutants there -- the missing graph read as drawn, and the explain answered for `Runner` alone -- were killed too: 3 of 3.
- **Every preview draws:** the 31 new previews (`KnowledgeScreen` 14, `GraphScreen` 11, `GraphSheets` 6) rendered server-side in a throwaway test in that clone, deleted after, without a throw and with no `undefined` or `NaN` -- their first state; their later states are paths the view-model tests and the walk drive.
- **`npm run check`**, exit 0, on the final tree after the review's fixes: `tsc --noEmit` clean; `unit` 1,504 of 1,504 in 102 files (was 1,419 in 97 after 1n; 1,500 before the review's fixes), the per-preset walk now 74 presets × 3 modes, still and after 30 ticks, both places read on every one, the graph in each of its views, both stand-in children's `unanswered` empty; `layering` 66 of 66 in 2 files (was 65), the token scan among them; `bundle` 17 of 17 in 5 files, the determinism test's two builds byte-identical; Playwright 175 passed in 3.8 minutes (was 153 after 1n; the review's fixes extended five of them and added none).
- **The acceptance criteria, each by a named test:** each of the six presets opens in the built file in all three `apogee` modes with the old regions, controls and wording, still, with no console error and no request but the file (18 Playwright tests) -- at `missing` the lists and the canvas empty with no words, as the old draws no missing state; at `config-typo` each place shows the CLI's refusal, an addition the old frames never drew -- and side by side above; both Knowledge tabs, both graph views, the prose graph, the More menu, the save panel, the dedupe sheet and the delete confirm work live from `file://` (four Playwright tests); every value on screen comes from a recorded or `*-handmade*` fixture (the fixture check, and the reads' tests on the recordings); the per-preset test, the view-model tests, the layering check and the staleness check pass (`npm run check`); this record names every place the port follows the prototype where the Mac app differs (the toasts, no search, no graph picker, no New graph…, no capture, ingest or export forms, the Files view, the file tree, Ask about this, Open in editor) and every recording that names another graph (above).
- **The guardrails, each by a named test:** the per-preset walk (`Presets.test.ts`, all 74 presets in all three modes, still and after 30 ticks; a planted record missing its fields and a node missing its file and line draw their absence in words, and a planted `undefined` in a card fails the walk naming its path); the view-model tests without React (asking ordered by `knowledge-query.json`'s scores to three places, the branch showing Rejected alone; the raw toggle showing the recorded text; a search showing `collection-query.txt`'s lines; a node's CALLED BY and CALLS from the recorded edges and Files' IMPORTED BY and IMPORTS; the save panel refusing an empty name and Save raising the recorded line; dedupe on `demo` Done only and on `notes` Merge them, merging, merged; delete asked, gone, Rebuild back; `GraphLayout` placing a planted 40-node document with no overlap inside the canvas); the layering check (`layering.test.ts`: a planted `import … from 'react'` in `graph/ViewModels/GraphViewModel.ts` and in `knowledge/ViewModels/RecordsViewModel.ts`, `knowledge` imported from `graph/Views/GraphScreen.tsx`, and `session` from `graph`, each failing with its sentence); the fixture check (`GraphFixtures.test.ts`: every file the six presets' answers look up exists and is imported, a design fixture says it is hand-made, the hand-made files hold their recordings' shapes; a planted name nobody imports, one gone from disk and one not named `*-handmade*` fail naming the preset and the file). The Code graph ⌘5 and Knowledge ⌘8 are proven through the key map (`AppViewModel.test.ts` presses each chord on the shell), the browser keeping ⌘1-⌘9 for its tabs, as 1e's and 1j's are.
- **Nothing installed under the prototype; no global install;** no `apogee` binary ran.
- **Unchanged:** nothing under `lib/src`, `lib/scripts`, `lib/design/spec` or `.github`. `lib/src/darwin` was not changed, so the Mac suite (`lib/scripts/cicd.sh --app darwin --test`) was not run.

**Not done.**
- **Your look.** Double-click `lib/design/main.html`, press Knowledge (⌘8 where the browser passes it) and Code graph (⌘5). On Records, press Ask with the field empty (the old question fills in), then pick a branch (it clears the question), show and hide the raw conversation; on Collections, notes current, search notes with the field empty, then pick each collection; on the graph, walk a neighbour, switch to Files and pick a file in the tree, open the prose graph, then More: export, dedupe on both graphs, delete and Rebuild; open `?start=graph:refused`, press OK, then click any other card or a file; press Ask about this. The Playwright tests stood in for this look during the run; 1s's report carries it too.
- **Your veto, if you want one,** on the decisions above, among them: the tab the composition's, the merged collections list and the knowledge collection's hand-made page, the branch clearing the question, the raw offered on every archived record, failures said in place, the card from the picture, explain only in an overtaken picture with the hand-made `_fetch` refusal, the layout's separation pass and grid, the layout over every relation, the edges' sides and the canvas's scroll, no build time in the header, the delete kept beside the reads, Rebuild's one-line stream, the save panel's exact names, Ask about this through the session's leave (and its absolute path), an empty field asking the old question, and notes' exports, report and Rebuild left unrecorded.
- **The capture that would align the graph's recordings** (export, report, delete and dedupe against `demo`, and a `graph update demo`): the user's run, after this item.
- **What the stand-in cannot do** (it keeps no state but which calls succeeded): a question, a search or a raw conversation nobody recorded, an Ask under a branch among them; a save under another name, and the notes graph's exports and report; notes' Rebuild -- each ends in the stand-in's words; a reload or another preset reads the graph as built.
- **The Mac's extra flows stay out** (the document's defaults): the graph's search, graph picker and New graph…; the capture, ingest and export forms, link, move and delete; a designed missing-CLI state -- new design, after this item.
- **The side-by-side's verdicts** are 1s's (`npm run parity`, `verdicts.json`), which this item does not build.
- **The previews' later states** are driven by the view-model tests and the walk, not by a browser render of each preview: a preview gallery is 1q's.
- **1q:27 says `src/`** where the tree uses `source/`; not this item's.
- **No commit**: the run's owner commits.
